Abstract: Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) imagery is widely used for flood monitoring due to its all-weather and day-night imaging capability. However, flood mapping using single-polarization SAR data remains challenging in complex environments where surface and volume scattering coexist. In this paper, we investigate the effectiveness of cross-polarization fusion of VV and VH SAR observations for improved flood mapping. A deep learning-based segmentation framework is employed to jointly exploit complementary information from VV and VH polarizations. To ensure a fair evaluation, three configurations are compared under identical training conditions: VV only, VH only, and fused VV-VH input. Performance is assessed using standard flood mapping metrics, including Intersection over Union (IoU) and F1-score, along with qualitative visual analysis. Experimental results demonstrate that VV-VH fusion consistently outperforms single-polarization models, particularly in vegetated and heterogeneous flood regions, leading to more accurate flood boundary delineation. The findings highlight the importance of cross-polarization SAR fusion for enhancing the reliability of SAR-based flood mapping in disaster monitoring applications.

</details>


### [110] [Heterogeneous Model Fusion for Privacy-Aware Multi-Camera Surveillance via Synthetic Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02169)
*Peggy Joy Lu,Wei-Yu Chen,Yao-Tsung Huang,Vincent Shin-Mu Tseng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HeroCrystal是一个面向多摄像机域自适应目标检测的隐私保护框架，包含生成、联邦和蒸馏三个阶段。通过单样本目标感知扩散生成模块、概率Faster R-CNN与动态模型对比策略，以及不一致类别整合算法，解决了数据隐私、类别不平衡和异构架构问题，在多个跨域检测基准上达到33.4% mAP的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在多摄像机跨域目标检测中面临三大挑战：1) 数据隐私问题——传统风格迁移需要大量目标域数据，违反隐私保护要求；2) 长尾类别退化——罕见对象类别因样本不足导致检测性能严重下降；3) 异构架构——不同客户端可能使用不同的模型架构，难以直接进行知识聚合。

Method: 三阶段框架：1) 生成阶段：提出单样本目标感知扩散生成模块，从一张目标域图像学习视觉风格，通过提示控制合成特定对象实例，实现隐私增强增强和可控稀有对象生成；2) 联邦阶段：客户端采用概率Faster R-CNN提升定位精度，配合动态模型对比策略抑制领域特定偏差；服务器端跨异构架构进行模型融合；3) 蒸馏阶段：提出不一致类别整合算法，解决客户端间的标签不一致性和架构异构性问题。

Result: 在多个跨域检测基准上，HeroCrystal相比现有隐私保护方法mAP提升+2.1%，达到33.4% mAP的新SOTA，超越多源域适应和联邦学习基线。

Conclusion: HeroCrystal有效解决了多摄像机AI监控系统中数据隐私、类别不均衡和模型异构等实际问题，展示了隐私保护跨域目标检测的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: We propose HeroCrystal, a novel privacy-preserving framework for multi-camera domain-adaptive object detection, addressing challenges such as data privacy, class imbalance, and heterogeneous architectures. Our framework consists of three key stages. In the Generated Stage, we introduce a one-shot, target-aware diffusion-based generation module that learns visual style from a single target-domain image while leveraging prompt-based control to synthesize specific object instances. Unlike conventional style transfer-based methods that require large target datasets and ignore semantic-level discrepancies, our approach enables privacy-preserving augmentation to reduce ethical concerns, and introduces controllable rare object generation to mitigate long-tailed category degradation. In the Federated Stage, we employ probabilistic Faster R-CNN on the client side to improve localization accuracy, and a dynamic model contrastive strategy to suppress domain-specific bias. The server side performs model fusion across heterogeneous architectures without accessing raw data. Finally, in the Distilled Stage, we propose an inconsistent categories integration algorithm to resolve label inconsistency and architecture heterogeneity across clients. Extensive experiments on multiple cross-domain detection benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms existing multi-source domain adaptation and federated learning baselines under multi-class, privacy-preserving settings. Our method improves mAP by +2.1% over prior privacy-preserving approaches and achieves a new state-of-the-art mAP of 33.4%, highlighting the effectiveness of HeroCrystal in enabling practical multi-camera AI surveillance systems.

</details>


### [111] [SlimDiffSR: Toward Lightweight and Efficient Remote Sensing Image Super-Resolution via Diffusion Model Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02198)
*Ce Wang,Zhenyu Hu,Wanjie Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SlimDiffSR，一种轻量级扩散遥感图像超分框架。通过不确定性引导的时间步分配策略构建单步教师模型，结合结构化剪枝（频率-方向可分离卷积、查询驱动全局聚合模块）和MMD蒸馏，实现最高200倍推理加速和20倍参数压缩，在保持竞争性感知质量的同时大幅提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在遥感图像超分中性能优异但计算成本极高，难以实际部署。已有单步扩散方法依赖固定时间步，未能充分利用遥感图像特性（稀疏高频细节、强方向模式、长程空间依赖），且缺乏针对遥感数据的轻量化设计。

Method: (1) 不确定性引导的时间步分配策略：将重建难度显式映射到扩散时间步，构建更强的单步教师模型；(2) 结构化剪枝策略：移除冗余语义模块，替换为频率可分离卷积、方向可分离卷积和查询驱动全局聚合模块，利用遥感数据特性；(3) MMD蒸馏：在对齐教师与学生模型特征分布，增强知识迁移。

Result: 在多个遥感基准上，相比多步扩散模型实现最高200倍推理加速和20倍参数量缩减，感知质量具有竞争力，效率明显优于现有轻量扩散基线。

Conclusion: 通过不确定性引导时间步分配、结构化剪枝和MMD蒸馏，实现了遥感图像超分中效率与质量的良好平衡，为扩散模型在遥感领域的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models have recently achieved remarkable performance in image super-resolution (SR), but their high computational cost limits practical deployment in remote sensing applications. To address this issue, we propose SlimDiffSR, a lightweight and efficient diffusion-based framework for real-world remote sensing image super-resolution. Unlike existing single-step diffusion methods that rely on fixed timesteps, we first introduce an uncertainty-guided timestep assignment strategy to construct a stronger single-step teacher model, where reconstruction difficulty is explicitly linked to diffusion timesteps, enabling adaptive generative strength. Building upon this teacher, we further present a structured pruning strategy tailored to remote sensing imagery, which systematically removes redundant semantic modules and replaces standard operations with lightweight designs, including frequency-separable convolution, direction-separable convolution, and a query-driven global aggregation module. These components explicitly exploit the unique characteristics of remote sensing data, such as sparse high-frequency details, strong directional patterns, and long-range spatial dependencies. To enhance knowledge transfer, we incorporate Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) into the distillation process to align feature distributions between the teacher and student models. Extensive experiments on multiple remote sensing benchmarks demonstrate that SlimDiffSR achieves a favorable balance between efficiency and reconstruction quality. In particular, it attains up to $200\times$ inference acceleration and a $20\times$ reduction in model parameters compared with multi-step diffusion models, while achieving competitive perceptual quality and clearly outperforming existing lightweight diffusion baselines in efficiency. The code is available at: https://github.com/wwangcece/SlimDiffSR.

</details>


### [112] [Super-resolution of airborne laser scanning point clouds for forest inventory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02201)
*Jinyuan Shao,Sangyoong Park,Chunxi Zhao,Ayman Habib,Songlin Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出3DFSR深度学习模型，通过体素CNN和U-Net架构同时提升ALS森林点云密度并降低噪声，在温带和寒带森林验证效果：F1分数从0.71提升至0.97，DBH估计RMSE从13.45cm降至6.43cm，跨平台泛化能力良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALS点云稀疏且噪声大，导致单木级森林清查（如树干定位和树木尺寸估计）不准确。

Method: 提出3DFSR模型，基于体素CNN和U-Net架构，同时对ALS森林点云进行点密度提升和噪声降低。

Result: 3DFSR在Chamfer距离0.249m和Hausdorff距离2.711m上优于SOTA；树干检测F1从0.71提升至0.97；DBH估计RMSE从13.45cm降至6.43cm；体积估计R²达0.95；适用于10-1700 points/m²密度范围，可跨平台泛化。

Conclusion: 3DFSR有效提升ALS点云质量，使原本适用于TLS/MLS的算法可直接用于增强后的点云，显著改善森林清查精度。

Abstract: Airborne Laser Scanning (ALS) can collect point clouds across large areas, enabling large-scale forest inventory. However, ALS point clouds are sparse and noisy, resulting in inaccurate individual-tree-level forest inventory, such as stem localization and tree size estimation. To overcome this problem, we propose a deep learning model, 3D Forest Super Resolution (3DFSR), to simultaneously improve point density and reduce noise for ALS forest point cloud. 3DFSR is a voxel-based CNN with a U-Net architecture. The proposed 3DFSR is evaluated on ALS point clouds collected in both temperate forests in the U.S. and boreal forests in Germany. Experimental results demonstrate that 3DFSR can generate finer point clouds of tree structure than other state-of-the-art point cloud super-resolution algorithms, achieving 0.249 m Chamfer Distance and 2.711 m Hausdorff Distance. Furthermore, to verify the effectiveness of 3DFSR point clouds in forest inventory, we conduct stem detection, DBH measurements, and stem reconstruction on both original ALS point clouds and 3DFSR enhanced point clouds. We find that stem detection and reconstruction algorithms developed for TLS/MLS point clouds can directly work on our 3DFSR point clouds, and DBH can be derived with circle-fitting method. F1 score of stem detection is improved from 0.71 on original ALS point clouds to 0.97 on 3DFSR point clouds; DBH estimation improves from 13.45 cm RMSE using allometric equations to 6.43 cm using circle fitting; comparing to stems reconstruction from MLS point clouds, stem reconstructed from 3DFSR point clouds has 0.170 m of Chamfer Distance and 0.377 m of Hausdorff Distance, and 0.95 R2 volume estimation. Finally, we find that the proposed 3DFSR is applicable to process point densities from 10 to 1700 points/m2; it also can be generalized across data collected from different LiDAR platforms without transfer learning.

</details>


### [113] [MultiSense-Pneumo: A Multimodal Learning Framework for Pneumonia Screening in Resource-Constrained Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02207)
*Dineth Jayakody,Pasindu Thenahandi,Chameli Dommanige*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiSense-Pneumo，一个整合症状描述、咳嗽音频、语音语言和胸部X光片的多模态肺炎筛查与分诊支持框架，可在低计算资源下离线运行，适用于资源匮乏环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺炎在资源匮乏地区造成重大疾病负担，现有临床评估依赖异质性多模态证据，但多数计算方法仅关注单一模态（主要是放射影像），缺乏整合多种临床信号的可部署系统。

Method: 系统结合了确定性症状分诊、基于LightGBM的声学分类、使用ResNet-18的领域对抗放射影像分析、基于Transformer的语音识别以及可解释的多模态融合算子。每个模态转化为归一化风险信号，通过融合算子聚合为统一的筛查估计。

Result: 实验结果表明放射影像通路在领域偏移下具有鲁棒性，同时揭示了声学信号在少数类召回率方面的局限性。

Conclusion: MultiSense-Pneumo是一个面向筛查与分诊支持的研究原型，而非临床验证的诊断系统，其设计目标是在标准笔记本硬件上全离线运行，适用于社区卫生工作者、乡村诊所和应急响应场景。

Abstract: Pneumonia remains a leading global cause of morbidity and mortality, particularly in low resource settings where access to imaging, laboratory testing, and specialist care is limited. Clinical assessment relies on heterogeneous evidence, including symptoms, respiratory patterns, and chest imaging, making screening inherently multimodal. However, many existing computational approaches remain unimodal and focus primarily on radiographs. In this work, we present MultiSense-Pneumo, a multimodal framework for pneumonia oriented screening and triage support that integrates structured symptom descriptors, cough audio, spoken language, and chest radiographs. The system combines deterministic symptom triage, LightGBM based acoustic classification, domain adversarial radiograph analysis using ResNet 18, transformer based speech recognition, and an interpretable multimodal fusion operator. Each modality is transformed into a normalized risk signal and aggregated into a unified screening estimate, enabling transparent and modular decision support. MultiSense-Pneumo is designed for real world deployment under modest computational constraints and can operate fully offline on standard laptop class hardware, making it suitable for community health workers, rural clinics, and emergency response settings. Experimental results demonstrate robustness of the radiograph pathway under domain shifts, while highlighting limitations in minority class recall for acoustic signals. MultiSense-Pneumo is intended as a research prototype for screening and triage support rather than a clinically validated diagnostic system.

</details>


### [114] [InfiltrNet: Dual-Branch CNN-Transformer Architecture for Brain Tumor Infiltration Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02230)
*S M Asif Hossain,Shruti Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InfiltrNet，一种结合CNN和Swin Transformer的双分支架构，通过跨注意力融合模块从多模态MRI预测胶质瘤的三区浸润风险图，在BraTS 2020和2025数据集上优于五个基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶质瘤会浸润MRI可见肿瘤边缘以外的周围组织，现有深度学习方法仅聚焦于可见肿瘤的分割，而非评估周围组织的浸润风险，这对手术规划和放疗至关重要。

Method: 提出InfiltrNet双分支架构：CNN编码器结合Swin Transformer编码器，通过跨注意力融合模块交互；基于距离变换的标签生成策略从BraTS标注推导浸润风险区；联合Dice-CrossEntropy和边界感知损失，并在中间解码层添加辅助监督头。

Result: 在BraTS 2020和BraTS 2025数据集上，InfiltrNet优于五种基线方法。GradCAM++和Occlusion敏感性可解释性分析证实模型关注临床上相关的瘤周区域。

Conclusion: InfiltrNet有效预测胶质瘤周围组织的浸润风险，为手术规划和放射治疗提供了更精准的决策支持。

Abstract: Gliomas are aggressive brain tumors that infiltrate surrounding tissue beyond the visible tumor margins observed on Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI). Predicting the spatial extent of this infiltration is essential for surgical planning and radiation therapy, yet existing deep learning approaches focus on segmenting the visible tumor rather than estimating infiltration risk in the surrounding tissue. This paper presents InfiltrNet, a novel dual-branch architecture that combines a convolutional neural network (CNN) encoder with a Swin Transformer encoder through cross-attention fusion modules to predict three-zone infiltration risk maps from multimodal MRI. A label generation strategy based on distance transforms is proposed to derive reproducible infiltration risk zones from standard Brain Tumor Segmentation (BraTS) annotations. InfiltrNet is trained with a combined Dice-CrossEntropy and boundary-aware loss augmented by auxiliary supervision heads at intermediate decoder levels. Extensive experiments on BraTS 2020 and BraTS 2025 demonstrate that InfiltrNet outperforms five established baselines. Explainability analysis using GradCAM++ and Occlusion sensitivity confirms that the model attends to clinically relevant peritumoral regions.

</details>


### [115] [Fine-Tuning Impairs the Balancedness of Foundation Models in Long-tailed Personalized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02247)
*Shihao Hou,Chikai Shang,Zhiheng Yang,Jiacheng Yang,Xinyi Shang,Junlong Gao,Yiqun Zhang,Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FedPuReL方法，通过梯度净化和残差学习解决长尾分布下的个性化联邦学习问题，在保持全局模型类别平衡的同时实现个性化，实验表明优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PFL方法在处理非独立同分布数据与长尾类分布共存场景时表现不佳，微调会侵蚀基础模型固有的类别平衡性，且传统个性化技术会将偏差传递给局部模型。

Method: 提出FedPuReL，包含两方面：(i) 利用零样本预测净化局部梯度，保持全局模型的类别平衡；(ii) 将个性化建模为冻结全局模型之上的残差校正。

Result: 在多种长尾场景下，FedPuReL在全局模型和个性化模型上均一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 梯度净化与残差学习能有效解决长尾PFL中类别不平衡与个性化偏差耦合的问题，为实际场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Personalized federated learning (PFL) with foundation models has emerged as a promising paradigm enabling clients to adapt to heterogeneous data distributions. However, real-world scenarios often face the co-occurrence of non-IID data and long-tailed class distributions, presenting unique challenges that remain underexplored in PFL. In this paper, we investigate this long-tailed personalized federated learning and observe that current methods suffer from two limitations: (i) fine-tuning degrades performance below zero-shot baselines due to the erosion of inherent class balance in foundation models; (ii) conventional personalization techniques further transfer this bias to local models through parameter or feature-level fusion. To address these challenges, we propose Federated Learning via Gradient Purification and Residual Learning (FedPuReL), which preserves balanced knowledge in the global model while enabling unbiased personalization. Specifically, we purify local gradients using zero-shot predictions to maintain a class-balanced global model, and model personalization as residual correction atop the frozen global model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FedPuReL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving superior performance on both global and personalized models across diverse long-tailed scenarios. The code is available at https://github.com/shihaohou/FedPuReL.

</details>


### [116] [Rethinking Electro-Optical Vision Foundation Models for Remote Sensing Retrieval: A Controlled Comparison with Generalist VFM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02283)
*Hyobin Park,Minseok Seo,Dong-Geol Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过受控对比实验发现，强通用视觉基础模型在遥感图像检索任务中与专用EO模型性能相当甚至更优，且跨场景泛化更稳定，表明EO专用预训练并不能保证更强的遥感表征，未来需更好利用遥感影像的物理、空间、光谱和地理特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感领域数据获取成本高、标注需要专家知识，视觉基础模型利用大规模无标注数据的优势在此领域尤为重要。然而，目前尚不清楚专为遥感设计的EO视觉基础模型是否真的优于强通用视觉基础模型，因此需要系统对比评估。

Method: 采用受控对比实验，在相同数据集、检索协议和评估指标下，对比代表性EO专用模型与通用视觉基础模型在遥感图像检索任务上的领域内性能和跨场景泛化能力。

Result: 强通用视觉基础模型在性能上与EO专用模型相当甚至更优；EO专用模型在跨场景评估中性能严重下降，而通用模型表现出更稳定的迁移能力。

Conclusion: EO专用预训练并不能保证更强的检索导向遥感表征，当前EO预训练策略存在局限性，未来EO视觉基础模型需更好地利用遥感影像的物理、空间、光谱和地理特性。

Abstract: Vision foundation models have attracted significant attention for their ability to leverage large-scale unlabeled visual data. This advantage is particularly important in remote sensing, where data acquisition is costly and annotation often requires expert knowledge. Recent electro-optical vision foundation models aim to learn domain-specific representations from remote sensing imagery, but it remains unclear whether they are more effective than strong generalist vision foundation models under retrieval-based evaluation. In this study, we conduct a controlled comparison between representative EO-specific and generalist vision foundation models for remote sensing image retrieval. Using the same datasets, retrieval protocol, and evaluation metric, we evaluate both in-domain performance and cross-scene generalization. Our results show that strong generalist vision foundation models are competitive with, and in some cases outperform, existing EO-specific models. Moreover, EO-specific models often suffer from substantial degradation under cross-scene evaluation, while generalist models show more stable transfer. These findings suggest that EO pretraining alone does not guarantee stronger retrieval-oriented remote sensing representations. We discuss the limitations of current EO-specific pretraining strategies and highlight the need for future EO vision foundation models to better exploit the physical, spatial, spectral, and geographic characteristics of remote sensing imagery.

</details>


### [117] [Beyond Known Objects: A Novel Framework for Open-Set Object Detection using Negative-Aware Norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02284)
*Yuchen Zhang,Yao Lu,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NAN-SPOT，一种无需重训基检测器的轻量级开放集目标检测框架，通过利用隐藏层中的负感知范数（NAN）度量来估计物体性（objectness），仅需数百张图像和几分钟训练即可达到甚至超过需要大量重训练的方法的性能。同时构建了更全面的COCO-Open数据集以支持评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放集目标检测方法通常需要大量重训练检测器来学习物体性，效率低且计算成本高。作者假设标准检测器在训练过程中已隐含了物体性线索，因此希望探索一种无需重训基检测器的轻量级方案。

Method: 提出NAN-SPOT框架，核心是利用预训练检测器隐藏层中的负感知范数（Negative-Aware Norm, NAN）作为物体性度量指标，仅需在数百张图像上进行分钟级训练即可估计物体性，无需重新训练基础目标检测器。同时构建了COCO-Open数据集，将未知对象标注从433个增加到1853个。

Result: NAN-SPOT在未知目标检测任务上性能优于需要大量重训练的方法，同时在已知目标检测性能上不妥协。新构建的COCO-Open数据集成为目前OSOD领域标注最全面的数据集。

Conclusion: NAN-SPOT验证了无需重训检测器即可实现有效的开放集目标检测，其高效性和鲁棒性使其成为自动驾驶开放世界感知的有前景方案。

Abstract: Open-Set Object Detection (OSOD) is crucial for autonomous driving, where perception systems must recognize and localize both known and previously unseen objects in complex, dynamic environments. While recent approaches deliver promising results, they often require retraining the detector extensively to learn objectness, which describes the likelihood that a bounding box tightly encloses a valid object, regardless of whether its category was learned during training. Deviating from existing work, we hypothesize that standard off-the-shelf detectors may already contain helpful cues for objectness, owing to their training on numerous and diverse known categories. Building on this idea, we propose NAN-SPOT, a training-light framework that does not require to retrain the base object detector and estimates objectness by leveraging a hidden layer metric called Negative-Aware Norm (NAN), requiring only minutes of training on just hundreds of images. To support comprehensive evaluation, we introduce COCO-Open, an expanded version of the existing COCO-Mixed dataset, increasing unknown object annotations from 433 to 1853, making it the most exhaustively labeled dataset for OSOD to the best of our knowledge. Experimental results demonstrate that NAN-SPOT achieves even better performance on unknown object detection than methods requiring heavy training, without compromising performance on known objects. This efficiency and robustness make NAN-SPOT a promising step towards open-world perception in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [118] [LabBuilder: Protocol-Grounded 3D Layout Generation for Interactable and Safe Laboratory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02288)
*Jianbao Cao,Zhangrui Zhao,Bohan Feng,Zixuan Hu,Rui Li,Haiyuan Wan,Chenxi Li,Jingyuan Li,Wenzhe Cai,Lei Bai,Wanli Ouyang,Lingyu Duan,Di Huang,Mingting Pan,Sha Zhang,Xinzhu Ma,Shixiang Tang,Dongzhan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LabBuilder是一个端到端系统，能从简洁文本说明生成并验证3D实验室布局，通过三个紧密耦合组件（LabForge、LabGen、LabTouchstone）实现功能有效且安全的实验环境设计，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化实验室的部署受限于安全且可执行的实验环境设计困难，现有3D场景生成方法主要针对家庭场景优化视觉合理性，忽视了科学实验所需的严格功能语义和安全约束。

Method: 提出端到端系统LabBuilder，包含三个组件：LabForge（构建标注资产元数据集和化学知识，将自然语言规格转为结构化协议）、LabGen（基于协议通过迭代约束感知优化策略合成实验室布局）、LabTouchstone（评估生成布局的统一基准）。

Result: 大量实验表明，LabBuilder显著优于现有最先进方法，生成的实验室环境不仅逼真，而且功能有效且安全，适用于复杂实验工作流。

Conclusion: LabBuilder通过将功能语义和安全约束纳入布局生成，成功解决了自动化实验室场景设计的核心瓶颈，为科学发现加速提供了可行的端到端解决方案。

Abstract: Automated laboratories hold the promise of accelerating scientific discovery, yet their deployment is bottlenecked by the difficulty of designing safe and executable environments. While simulator-based design offers scalability, existing 3D scene generation methods are primarily tailored for household settings, optimizing for visual plausibility while neglecting the rigorous functional semantics and safety constraints essential for scientific experimentation. We present LabBuilder, an end-to-end system that generates and verifies 3D laboratory layouts from concise textual specifications. It operates through three tightly coupled components: LabForge first curates a meta-dataset of annotated assets and chemical knowledge, translating natural language specifications into structured protocols; building on these protocols, LabGen synthesizes laboratory layouts via an iterative, constraint-aware optimization strategy; finally, LabTouchstone evaluates the resulting layouts as a unified benchmark. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LabBuilder significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods, producing laboratory environments that are not only realistic but also functionally valid and safe for complex experimental workflows.

</details>


### [119] [A Hybrid Approach for Closing the Sim2real Appearance Gap in Game Engine Synthetic Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02291)
*Stefanos Pasios*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用先进扩散模型（FLUX.2-4B Klein）增强合成数据集逼真度的能力，并将其与传统图像到图像翻译模型（REGEN）进行比较，提出了一种混合方法，实验表明混合方法在保持语义一致性的同时能获得更好的视觉真实感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代游戏引擎虽能生成大量视觉合成数据集，但合成图像与真实图像之间仍存在显著的sim2real外观差距，限制了合成数据集在现实世界应用中的使用。

Method: 1) 使用SOTA扩散模型FLUX.2-4B Klein增强合成数据集的真实感；2) 与传统图像到图像翻译模型REGEN进行性能比较；3) 提出结合扩散模型（几何和材质变换能力强）与图像到图像翻译技术（分布匹配能力强）的混合方法。

Result: REGEN模型在提升真实感方面优于FLUX.2-4B Klein；将两种模型结合使用比单独使用任一模型能获得更好的视觉真实感，同时保持语义一致性。

Conclusion: 混合方法能够有效弥合sim2real外观差距，代码已开源。

Abstract: Video game engines have been an important source for generating large volumes of visual synthetic datasets for training and evaluating computer vision algorithms that are to be deployed in the real world. While the visual fidelity of modern game engines has been significantly improved with technologies such as ray-tracing, a notable sim2real appearance gap between the synthetic and the real-world images still remains, which limits the utilization of synthetic datasets in real-world applications. In this letter, we investigate the ability of a state-of-the-art image generation and editing diffusion model (FLUX.2-4B Klein) to enhance the photorealism of synthetic datasets and compare its performance against a traditional image-to-image translation model (REGEN). Furthermore, we propose a hybrid approach that combines the strong geometry and material transformations of diffusion-based methods with the distribution-matching capabilities of image-to-image translation techniques. Through experiments, it is demonstrated that REGEN outperforms FLUX.2-4B Klein and that by combining both FLUX.2-4B Klein and REGEN models, better visual realism can be achieved compared to using each model individually, while maintaining semantic consistency. The code is available at: https://github.com/stefanos50/Hybrid-Sim2Real

</details>


### [120] [Open-access model for detecting openly dumped dispersed municipal solid waste from crowdsourced UAV imagery in Sub-Saharan Africa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02316)
*Steffen Knoblauch,Ram Kumar Muthusamy,Luis M. A. Bettencourt,Costas Velis,Pierre Chrzanowski,Edward Charles Anderson,Pete Masters,Innocent Maholi,Antonio Inguane,Levi Szamek,Alexander Zipf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的开源模型，利用众包无人机影像自动检测撒哈拉以南非洲地区的分散式露天垃圾堆，模型在10个国家29个区域验证表现优异，为城市固废管理提供低成本、高精度的空间监测工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 撒哈拉以南非洲快速城市化地区，分散式非法垃圾倾倒问题严峻，但缺乏高分辨率数据集进行空间监测，亟需可扩展的低成本自动化检测工具。

Method: 利用众包无人机影像，基于手动标注的图像瓦片训练深度学习模型，在10个国家29个区域进行跨环境验证。

Result: 模型在检测分散式露天垃圾方面表现优异；垃圾堆积呈现异质性模式（沿水道热点与城市散布并存）；垃圾积累与人口密度及局部基础设施缺失强相关，与区域发展宏观指标相关性较弱。

Conclusion: 该开源模型可作为即用型工具，使地方从业者无需深厚技术背景即可将无人机影像转化为可行动洞察，支持精准干预，改善撒哈拉以南非洲的城市固废管理。

Abstract: Managing municipal solid waste in rapidly urbanizing Sub-Saharan Africa remains challenging due to dispersed informal dumping and limited high-resolution datasets for spatial monitoring. We present an open-access deep learning model for automated detection of openly dumped dispersed solid waste via crowdsourced UAV imagery, trained and evaluated across 29 regions in 10 countries, encompassing diverse environmental contexts. A deep learning model trained on manually annotated image tiles achieved excellent performance in detecting openly dumped dispersed solid waste across all study regions. Predicted distributions reveal heterogeneous accumulation patterns, ranging from localized hotspots - often along waterways, where waste can exacerbate flood and public health risks - to more dispersed litter across urban areas. Waste accumulation is most strongly associated with population density and indicators of lack of local infrastructure access, whereas its relationship with broader measures of regional development is weaker, highlighting the importance of fine-scale data for understanding localized waste dynamics. By releasing the model, this study provides a ready-to-use tool for UAV imagery collected by municipalities and local mapping communities, enabling openly dumped dispersed solid waste monitoring without extensive technical expertise. This approach empowers local practitioners to convert UAV imagery into actionable insights, supporting targeted interventions and improved municipal solid waste management across Sub-Saharan Africa.

</details>


### [121] [Improving Imbalanced Multi-Label Chest X-Ray Diagnosis via CBAM-Enhanced CNN Backbones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02328)
*Duy Nguyen Huu,Duy Hoang Khuong,Ngu Huynh Cong Viet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将卷积块注意力模块（CBAM）集成到传统CNN模块中，以增强胸部X光片多标签分类性能，在ChestXray14数据集上实现了0.8695的平均AUC，优于多个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统胸部X光片诊断耗时且依赖专家知识，深度学习虽提升了自动化特征提取效率，但仍面临类别不平衡和多病变共存定位等挑战。

Method: 受CBAM在特征精炼方面的优势和CNN模块在特征提取方面的能力启发，将CBAM集成到传统CNN模块中，形成增强型特征提取与分类架构。

Result: 在ChestXray14数据集上取得0.8695的平均AUC，优于多个最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: CBAM与CNN的集成策略能有效提升多标签胸部疾病分类性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Chest radiography is a widely used imaging modality for thoracic disease diagnosis, yet its conventional interpretation remains time-consuming and heavily dependent on expert knowledge. While deep learning has improved diagnostic efficiency through automated feature extraction, challenges such as class imbalance and the localization of multiple co-existing pathologies remain unsolved. In this paper, inspired by the strength of Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM) in feature refinement and the capability of CNN blocks in feature extraction, we propose a strategy to integrate CBAM into traditional CNN blocks to enhance performance in multi-label classification tasks. Our method achieves a mean AUC of 0.8695 on ChestXray14 dataset, outperforming several state-of-the-art baselines.Our source code is available at: https://github.com/NNNguyenDuyyy/FETC_CBAM_Enhanced_CNN.git

</details>


### [122] [Enhancing Multimodal In-Context Learning via Inductive-Deductive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02378)
*Haoyu Wang,Haonan Wang,Yuyan Chen,Jun Chen,Gang Liu,Qian Wang,Jiahong Yan,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析了视觉语言模型（VLM）在上下文学习（ICL）中的脆弱性根源——归纳差距（inductive gap），即模型常通过错误推理得出正确答案，且难以从示范中提取一致规则。作者提出一个将多模态ICL重构为归纳-演绎过程的框架，包含基于相似性的视觉token压缩、动态注意力再平衡、思维链推理范式以及结合监督微调与强化学习的辅助学习流程。在8个基准上验证了对多个开源VLM的一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在ICL场景下表现脆弱，根本原因在于存在归纳差距（inductive gap）：模型常基于错误推理得到正确答案，且难以从示范中提取一致的、可迁移的规则。此外，还存在视觉层面的两大障碍：冗余视觉token淹没文本线索，以及注意力分布偏斜导致后续图像信息被忽视。

Method: 提出了一个将多模态ICL重构为归纳-演绎过程的框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）基于相似性的视觉token压缩模块，用于过滤冗余图像块；（2）动态注意力再平衡机制，使注意力在全部图像间均衡分布；（3）思维链（CoT）范式，显式引导模型先分析示例、推导可泛化规则，再应用于查询。辅助学习流程结合了监督微调与基于可验证奖励的强化学习，以强化忠实引用和噪声过滤。

Result: 在8个涵盖视觉感知、逻辑推理、STEM问题和讽刺检测的基准上，对于多个开源VLM，所提框架相比标准ICL基线取得了一致且显著的改进。

Conclusion: 多模态ICL的脆弱性源于归纳差距，通过将ICL重构为结构化的归纳-演绎过程，并配合视觉token压缩、注意力再平衡和CoT推理，可以有效提升VLM在多模态任务中的归纳推理能力，具备为模型赋予真正归纳能力的潜力。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) allows large models to adapt to tasks using a few examples, yet its extension to vision-language models (VLMs) remains fragile. Our analysis reveals that the fundamental limitation lies in an inductive gap, models often produce correct answers from flawed reasoning, while struggling to extract consistent rules across demonstrations. This gap is further exacerbated by two visual-level obstacles: an overwhelming proportion of redundant visual tokens that obscure textual cues, and a skewed attention distribution that favors the initial image at the expense of subsequent context. To address these issues, we introduce a framework that restructures multimodal ICL as a principled inductive-deductive process. The framework incorporates a similarity-based visual token compression module to filter out redundant patches, a dynamic attention rebalancing mechanism to distribute focus equitably across all images, and a chain-of-thought paradigm that explicitly guides the model to analyze individual examples, derive a generalizable rule, and then apply it to the query. An auxiliary learning pipeline combines supervised fine-tuning with reinforcement learning using verifiable rewards to reinforce faithful citation and noise filtering. Evaluations across eight benchmarks covering visual perception, logical reasoning, STEM problems, and sarcasm detection demonstrate consistent and significant improvements over standard ICL baselines for multiple open-source VLMs, highlighting the potential of equipping models with genuine inductive capabilities in multimodal settings.

</details>


### [123] [UnGAP: Uncertainty-Guided Affine Prompting for Real-Time Crack Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02380)
*Conghui Li,Huanyu He,Xin Wang,Weiyao Lin,Chern Hong Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UnGAP提出了一种闭环机制，将aleatoric不确定性从被动指标转化为主动视觉提示，通过不确定性引导的特征调制器（UPFM）进行像素级特征校准，解决了异方差建模中高方差导致梯度衰减的结构优化病理问题，同时实现实时裂纹分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前不确定性感知方法仅将不确定性估计作为事后分析的被动终点，未能将信息反馈用于优化特征表示；且独立像素级异方差建模在裂纹分割中虽适合，但存在高预测方差削弱梯度、导致模型忽略困难样本并欠拟合复杂边界的结构优化病理。

Method: 提出UnGAP框架，核心包括：1) 不确定性提示特征调制器（UPFM），将aleatoric不确定性作为主动视觉提示，通过像素级仿射变换动态校准特征分布，将高方差从梯度抑制信号转化为特征修正信号；2) 边界感知检测头进一步约束预测精度。

Result: 实验表明UnGAP在保证实时推理速度的同时实现了优越的分割精度，验证了将不确定性从被动度量转化为主动校准工具的有效性。

Conclusion: UnGAP成功建立了不确定性估计与特征学习之间的闭环机制，将病理性的高方差转化为建设性的特征修正信号，为不确定性感知的裂纹分割提供了新范式。

Abstract: Real-time crack segmentation is vital for structural health monitoring but is plagued by aleatoric uncertainties arising from varying lighting, blur, and texture ambiguity. Current uncertainty-aware approaches typically treat uncertainty estimation as a passive endpoint for post-hoc analysis, failing to close the loop by feeding this information back to refine feature representations. We contend that independent pixel-wise heteroscedastic modeling is uniquely suited for crack segmentation, as cracks are defined by fine-grained local gradients rather than the global semantic coherence relied upon in general object segmentation. However, this approach suffers from a structural optimization pathology: high predicted variance attenuates loss gradients, effectively causing the model to ignore difficult samples and under-fit complex boundaries. To address these challenges, we propose UnGAP, a novel framework that establishes a closed-loop mechanism between uncertainty estimation and feature learning. Central to our approach is the Uncertainty-Prompted Feature Modulator (UPFM), which treats aleatoric uncertainty as an active visual prompt rather than a mere output. UPFM dynamically calibrates feature distributions through pixel-wise affine transformations. Crucially, this mechanism mitigates the heteroscedastic pathology by transforming high variance, which would otherwise indicate gradient suppression, into a constructive signal for stronger feature rectification in ambiguous regions. Additionally, a boundary-aware detection head is introduced to further constrain prediction precision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UnGAP balances superior segmentation accuracy with real-time inference speed, effectively validating the benefit of transforming uncertainty from a passive metric into an active calibration tool.

</details>


### [124] [FEAT: Fashion Editing and Try-On from Any Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02393)
*Soye Kwon,Keonyoung Lee,Dahuin Jung,Jaekoo Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FEAT是一种基于解耦双注入和正交引导噪声融合的时尚编辑与虚拟试穿方法，支持使用多样化设计来源（包括艺术品、抽象图像、自然照片等非服装类图像）对完整服饰（含配饰）进行编辑和试穿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法局限于服装类图像作为设计来源，无法利用艺术品、抽象图像、自然照片等创意设计素材，且不支持包含配饰的完整着装编辑与试穿。

Method: 提出解耦双注入（DDI），将服装与非服装设计来源通过内容和风格解耦选择性注入设计线索；提出正交引导噪声融合（OGNF），通过正交投影去除残留服装并使用区域特定噪声策略实现服装和配饰的虚拟试穿。

Result: 在设计灵活性、提示一致性和视觉真实性方面达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: FEAT有效扩展了时尚设计输入源的多样性，突破传统服装图像限制，并首次支持包含配饰的完整着装虚拟试穿。

Abstract: Fashion design aims to express a designer's creative intent and to depict how garments interact with the human body. Recent methods condition on multimodal inputs to support garment editing and virtual try-on. However, existing methods still (i) confine design to garment-related images, excluding creative design sources such as artwork, abstract imagery, and natural photographs, and (ii) cannot support complete outfits, including accessories. We present FEAT (Fashion Editing And Try-On from Any Design), a method that enables editing and try-on across garments and accessories using diverse design sources. To achieve this, we introduce Disentangled Dual Injection (DDI). It takes both apparel and non-apparel design sources and selectively injects design cues via content and style disentanglement. Furthermore, we propose Orthogonal-Guided Noise Fusion (OGNF), a training-free mechanism that removes residual garments via orthogonal projection and applies region-specific noise strategies to enable virtual try-on for both garments and accessories. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FEAT achieves state-of-the-art performance in design flexibility, prompt consistency, and visual realism.

</details>


### [125] [DirectEdit: Step-Level Accurate Inversion for Flow-Based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02417)
*Desong Yang,Mang Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DirectEdit提出了一种无需额外神经函数评估的训练免费图像编辑方法，通过直接对齐前向路径消除逆过程累积漂移，并结合注意力特征注入与多分支掩码引导噪声混合机制，在保持重构保真度的同时实现高效精确的图像编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练免费图像编辑方法因逆过程使用不匹配时间步的噪声潜变量，导致累积漂移，从根本上限制了重构保真度。本文旨在消除这一固有重构误差，无需增加额外计算成本。

Method: 1) 系统分析流变换器中的逆过程；2) 直接对齐前向路径而非修正逆路径，实现精确重构与可靠特征共享；3) 引入基于注意力特征注入的保持机制；4) 多分支掩码引导噪声混合策略。

Result: 在多种场景下的广泛实验表明，DirectEdit实现了高效准确的图像编辑，性能超越当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: DirectEdit通过直接对齐前向路径的创新思路，在不引入额外NFE的情况下解决了逆过程漂移问题，为训练免费图像编辑提供了更优的保真度-可编辑性平衡方案。

Abstract: With recent advancements in large-scale pre-trained text-to-image (T2I) models, training-free image editing methods have demonstrated remarkable success. Typically, these methods involve adding noise to a clean image via an inversion process, followed by separate denoising steps for the reconstruction and editing paths during the forward process. However, since the reconstruction path is approximated using noisy latents from mismatched timesteps, existing methods inevitably suffer from accumulated drift, which fundamentally limits reconstruction fidelity. To address this challenge, we systematically analyze the inversion process within the flow transformer and propose DirectEdit, a simple yet effective editing method that eliminates the inherent reconstruction error without introducing additional neural function evaluations (NFEs). Unlike most prior works that attempt to rectify the inversion path, DirectEdit focuses on directly aligning the forward paths, enabling precise reconstruction and reliable feature sharing. Furthermore, we introduce a preservation mechanism based on attention feature injection and multi-branch mask-guided noise blending, which effectively balances fidelity and editability. Extensive experiments across diverse scenarios demonstrate that DirectEdit achieves efficient and accurate image editing, delivering superior performance that outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Code and examples are available at https://desongyang.github.io/Directedit.

</details>


### [126] [Mixture Prototype Flow Matching for Open-Set Supervised Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02438)
*Fuyun Wang,Yuanzhi Wang,Xu Guo,Sujia Huang,Tong Zhang,Dan Wang,Hui Yan,Xin Liu,Zhen Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出混合原型流匹配（MPFM）框架，用于开放集监督异常检测。通过将正态特征分布连续变换到结构化的高斯混合原型空间，并引入互信息最大化正则化器，解决了现有原型方法因单峰高斯先验无法捕捉多模态特性导致决策边界模糊的问题，在多种基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于原型的开放集监督异常检测方法通常使用单峰高斯先验对正态数据进行建模，无法捕捉数据固有的多模态特性，导致决策边界模糊，检测性能受限。

Method: 提出混合原型流匹配（MPFM）框架，学习从正态特征分布到结构化高斯混合原型空间的连续变换。不同于传统流方法使用单一速度向量，MPFM将速度场显式建模为高斯混合先验，每个分量对应不同的正态类别。同时引入互信息最大化正则化器（MIMR）防止原型坍缩并最大化正常-异常可分离性。

Result: 在多种基准数据集上，单异常和多异常设定下均取得最先进的检测性能。

Conclusion: MPFM通过多模态原型建模和互信息正则化有效提升了开放集异常检测的性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Open-set supervised anomaly detection (OSAD) aims to identify unseen anomalies using limited anomalous supervision. However, existing prototype-based methods typically model normal data via a unimodal Gaussian prior, failing to capture inherent multi-modality and resulting in blurred decision boundaries. To address this, we propose Mixture Prototype Flow Matching (MPFM), a framework that learns a continuous transformation from normal feature distributions to a structured Gaussian mixture prototype space. Departing from traditional flow-based approaches that rely on a single velocity vector, MPFM explicitly models the velocity field as a Gaussian mixture prior where each component corresponds to a distinct normal class. This design facilitates mode-aware and semantically coherent distribution transport. Furthermore, we introduce a Mutual Information Maximization Regularizer (MIMR) to prevent prototype collapse and maximize normal-anomaly separability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MPFM achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse benchmarks under both single- and multi-anomaly settings.

</details>


### [127] [Anomaly-Preference Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02439)
*Fuyun Wang,Yuanzhi Wang,Xu Guo,Sujia Huang,Tong Zhang,Dan Wang,Hui Yan,Xin Liu,Zhen Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出异常偏好优化（Anomaly Preference Optimization），将异常样本生成重构为偏好学习问题，通过隐式偏好对齐机制和时域感知容量分配模块，在有限数据条件下同时提升生成样本的真实性与多样性，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常样本生成方法在有限数据下难以兼顾保真度（fidelity）与多样性（diversity），常受分布对齐偏差和过拟合问题困扰。

Method: 1) 隐式偏好对齐机制（Implicit Preference Alignment）：以真实异常样本为正参考，从去噪轨迹偏差中直接提取优化信号，无需人工标注；2) 时域感知容量分配模块（Time-Aware Capacity Allocation）：沿扩散时间线动态分配模型容量，高噪声阶段优先结构多样性，低噪声阶段增强细粒度保真度；3) 分层采样策略（Hierarchical Sampling Strategy）：推理时调节一致性-保真度权衡，实现生成控制。

Result: 在真实性和多样性两项指标上均显著超越现有基线方法，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 将异常生成转化为偏好学习问题，通过隐式对齐与动态容量分配，有效解决了有限数据下保真度与多样性的矛盾。

Abstract: Synthesizing realistic and diverse anomalous samples from limited data is vital for robust model generalization. However, existing methods struggle to reconcile fidelity and diversity, often hampered by distribution misalignment and overfitting, respectively.To mitigate this, we introduce Anomaly Preference Optimization,a novel paradigm that reformulates anomaly generation as a preference learning problem.Central to our approach is an implicit preference alignment mechanism that leverages real anomalies as positive references, deriving optimization signals directly from denoising trajectory deviations without requiring costly human annotation. Furthermore, we propose a Time-Aware Capacity Allocation module that dynamically distributes model capacity along the diffusion timeline,prioritizing structural diversity during highnoise phases while enhancing fine-grained fidelity in low-noise stages. During inference, a hierarchical sampling strategy modulates the coherencealignment trade-off, enabling precise control over generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that significantly outperforms existing baselines,achieving state-of-the-art performance in both realism and diversity.

</details>


### [128] [ExpoCM: Exposure-Aware One-Step Generative Single-Image HDR Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02464)
*Aoyu Liu,Zhen Liu,Ziyi Wang,Dian Chen,Bing Zeng,Shuaicheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ExpoCM是一种单步一致性生成式HDR重建框架，通过曝光感知的概率流ODE轨迹和软曝光掩膜，在单次前向推理中同时实现过曝区域细节修复、欠曝区域噪声抑制和正常区域结构保持，无需知识蒸馏，在HDR-REAL、HDR-EYE和AIM2025基准上达到SOTA，推理速度相比DDPM提升400倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图像HDR重建因过曝区域细节饱和和欠曝区域噪声放大而高度病态。现有扩散方法忽略了退化与曝光水平的依赖性，且迭代采样计算成本高，亟需高效且曝光感知的解决方案。

Method: 1) 将HDR重建重新表述为概率流ODE，构建曝光感知的一致性轨迹；2) 构建软曝光掩膜，将LDR图像划分为过曝、欠曝和正常区域；3) 设计区域条件一致性轨迹，单步无蒸馏推理；4) 引入CIE L*a*b*空间的曝光引导亮度-色度损失，赋予曝光感知权重。

Result: 在HDR-REAL、HDR-EYE和AIM2025基准上达到SOTA保真度和感知准确度；推理速度比DDPM（1000步）快400倍以上，比DDIM（50步）快20倍以上。

Conclusion: ExpoCM通过曝光感知一致性轨迹和专用损失函数，在单步推理中实现了高效的HDR重建，显著降低了计算开销的同时保持了高质量结果，为扩散模型在底层视觉任务中的实际部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: Single-image HDR reconstruction aims to recover high dynamic range radiance from a single low dynamic range (LDR) input, but remains highly ill-posed due to detail saturation in over-exposed regions and noise amplification in under-exposed areas. While recent diffusion-based approaches offer powerful generative priors, they often overlook the exposure-dependent nature of the degradation and incur substantial computational costs from iterative sampling. To address these challenges, we propose ExpoCM, a novel one-step generative HDR reconstruction framework that reformulates HDR reconstruction as a Probability Flow ODE (PF-ODE) and constructs exposure-aware consistency trajectories via exposure-dependent perturbations. Specifically, a soft exposure mask is first constructed to separate the LDR image into over-, under-, and well-exposed regions. Based on this partition, region-conditioned consistency trajectories are designed to hallucinate saturated details, suppress noise in dark regions, and preserve reliable structures within a single, distillation-free inference step. To further enhance perceptual quality, we introduce an Exposure-guided Luminance-Chromaticity Loss in the CIE~$\text{L}^*\text{a}^*\text{b}^*$ space, which assigns exposure-aware weights to luminance and chromaticity components, effectively mitigating brightness bias and color drift. Extensive experiments on the HDR-REAL, HDR-EYE, and AIM2025 benchmarks demonstrate that ExpoCM achieves state-of-the-art fidelity and perceptual accuracy, while enabling over 400$\times$ and 20$\times$ faster inference compared to DDPM (1000 steps) and DDIM (50 steps), respectively.

</details>


### [129] [MooD: An Efficient VA-Driven Affective Image Editing Framework via Fine-Grained Semantic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02521)
*Xinyi Yin,Yiduo Wang,Tingqi Hu,Meicong Si,Yunyun Shi,Shi Chen,Hao Wang,Junxiao Xue,Xuecheng Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MooD框架，首个直接利用连续效价-唤醒（VA）值实现细粒度、高效情感图像编辑的方法，结合VA感知检索、视觉迁移和语义引导，并构建了VA注释数据集AffectSet，实验证明其在情感可控性和视觉保真度上表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感图像编辑方法忽视推理效率且依赖离散情感表示，难以捕捉复杂细微的人类情感，限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出MooD框架，包含三部分：1) VA-Aware检索策略，连接模糊情感值与具体视觉语义；2) 视觉迁移与语义引导相结合实现可控情感编辑；3) 构建VA注释数据集AffectSet支持模型优化与评估。

Result: 定性和定量实验表明MooD在情感可控性和视觉保真度上均优于现有方法，同时保持高效率；消融实验揭示了关键设计因素。

Conclusion: MooD是首个直接利用连续VA值进行细粒度情感图像编辑的框架，有效解决了离散表示的限制和效率问题，代码与数据将开源。

Abstract: Affective image editing (AIE) aims to edit visual content to evoke target emotions. However, existing methods often overlook inference efficiency and predominantly depend on discrete emotion representations, which to some extent limits their practical applicability and makes it challenging to capture complex and subtle human emotions. To tackle these gaps, we propose MooD, the first framework that directly leverages continuous Valence-Arousal (VA) values for fine-grained and efficient AIE. Specifically, we first introduce a VA-Aware retrieval strategy to bridge vague affective values and concrete visual semantics. Building upon this, MooD integrates visual transfer and semantic guidance to achieve controllable AIE. Furthermore, we construct AffectSet, a VA-annotated dataset to support model optimization and evaluation. Extensive qualitative and quantitative experimental results demonstrate that our MooD achieves superior performance in both affective controllability and visual fidelity while maintaining high efficiency. A series of ablation studies further reveal the crucial factors of our design. Our code and data will be made publicly open soon.

</details>


### [130] [TemPose-TF-ASF: Two-Stage Bidirectional Stroke Context Fusion for Badminton Stroke Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02558)
*Tzu-Yu Liu,Duan-Shin Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TemPose-TF-ASF（Adjacent-Stroke Fusion），一种通过显式建模前后击球的双向时序依赖关系来增强羽毛球击球预测的上下文感知方法。采用两阶段训练推理策略，将基线模型的初步预测作为时序上下文，联合优化ASF模块与分类器。在大规模比赛数据集上，该方法在Accuracy和Macro-F1上持续优于基线，且可无缝集成到现有SOTA模型中，展现出强迁移性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有羽毛球击球预测方法难以建模丰富的时序上下文信息，限制了细粒度运动分析和战术决策支持的效果。

Method: 提出TemPose-TF-ASF，通过两阶段训练推理策略：第一阶段使用基线模型（TemPose）生成初步击球预测；第二阶段将这些预测作为估计的时序上下文，联合优化ASF模块（显式建模前后击球的依赖关系）和分类器。

Result: 在大规模羽毛球比赛数据集上，该方法在Accuracy和Macro-F1指标上持续优于基线模型及其变体；将ASF集成到其他先进方法中也取得了显著性能提升，验证了方法的强转移性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模双向时序击球依赖关系，ASF可无缝集成到现有SOTA模型中，有效提升击球预测的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Accurate badminton stroke prediction is crucial for fine-grained sports analysis and tactical decision support. However, existing methods struggle to model rich temporal context. This paper introduces \emph{TemPose-TF-ASF (Adjacent-Stroke Fusion)}, a context-aware extension of \emph{TemPose}. It enhances stroke recognition by incorporating stroke-type information from both preceding and subsequent strokes. A two-stage training and inference strategy is adopted. Preliminary predictions from the baseline model are reused as estimated temporal context. These predictions guide the joint optimization of the \emph{ASF} module and the classifier. By explicitly modeling bidirectional temporal stroke dependencies, the proposed method can be seamlessly integrated into existing state-of-the-art models. Experiments on a large-scale badminton match dataset show consistent improvements over the baseline and its variants in terms of Accuracy and Macro-F1. Moreover, integrating \emph{ASF} into other advanced methods yields notable performance gains. These results demonstrate strong transferability and generalization capability.

</details>


### [131] [Automated In-the-Wild Data Collection for Continual AI Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02567)
*Thanasis Pantsios,Dimitrios Karageorgiou,Christos Koutlis,George Karantaidis,Olga Papadopoulou,Symeon Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据驱动的持续适应框架，结合野外数据（通过事实核查文章自动构建）和生成器驱动数据，用于AI生成图像检测器在演化环境中的更新，在两种SOTA检测器上分别提升+9.14%和+8%的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器在分布偏移和面对新出现的生成模型时性能严重下降，缺乏有效的持续更新机制。

Method: 提出数据中心的持续适应框架：(1) 通过事实核查文章检索构建自动化的弱监督野外数据集；(2) 在训练中引入少量生成器驱动数据以适配新模型；(3) 结合两者在持续学习框架下实现鲁棒适应并缓解灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在两种SOTA检测器上分别取得+9.14%和+8%的平均准确率提升。

Conclusion: 野外数据和生成器驱动数据对检测器适应演化环境均至关重要，结合持续学习框架可有效缓解灾难性遗忘并提升检测鲁棒性。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of generative Artificial Intelligence (AI) has introduced significant challenges for reliable AI-generated image detection. Existing detectors often suffer from performance degradation under distribution shifts and when encountering newly emerging generative models. In this work, we propose a data-centric continual adaptation framework for updating detectors in evolving environments. We show that both in-the-wild data and generator-driven data are essential for adapting detectors. We introduce an automated, weakly supervised pipeline for constructing in-the-wild datasets through fact-check article retrieval. Additionally, we demonstrate that incorporating even a small amount of generator-driven data during training enables effective adaptation to newly emerging models, while combining it with in-the-wild data within a continual learning framework enables robust adaptation and mitigates catastrophic forgetting. Extensive experiments on two state-of-the-art detectors show significant improvements of +9.14% and +8% in average accuracy, respectively.

</details>


### [132] [Self-Supervised Spatial And Zero-Shot Angular Super-Resolution by Spatial-Angular Implicit Representation For Rotating-View SNR-Efficient Diffusion MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02575)
*Yinzhe Wu,Hongyu Rui,Fanwen Wang,Jiahao Huang,Zi Wang,Guang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自监督空间-角度隐式神经表示（SA-INR），从每个扩散方向单视角数据重建高分辨率dMRI，实现空间超分和角度超分，显著加速扫描并提升下游DTI精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转视角厚层采集在介观dMRI中SNR高效，但需大量旋转视角满足奈奎斯特采样，导致扫描时间过长。现有方法无法从单视角数据高效重建高分辨率dMRI。

Method: 基于MLP的隐式神经表示，以b=0结构先验和扩散方向（通过FiLM调制）为条件，在各项异性输入上进行端到端自监督训练，同时学习连续q空间表示。

Result: 模拟数据上训练方向PSNR达34.82 dB，未见方向达33.08 dB；合成的角度数据提升了下游DTI模型拟合的定量精度。

Conclusion: SA-INR打破了经典采样极限，实现了快速、定量的高分辨率dMRI。

Abstract: Rotating-view thick-slice acquisition is highly SNR-efficient for mesoscale diffusion MRI (dMRI) but requires numerous rotating views to satisfy Nyquist sampling, resulting in long scan time. We propose a self-supervised Spatial-Angular Implicit Neural Representation (SA-INR) that reconstructs high-resolution dMRI from a single view per diffusion direction, representing a massive acceleration. Our model, an MLP conditioned on a b=0 structural prior and the b-direction via FiLM, is trained end-to-end on the anisotropic input. The framework not only accurately reconstructs the trained b-directions (spatial SR) but also learns a continuous q-space representation, enabling high-fidelity "zero-shot" synthesis of unseen b-directions (angular SR). On simulated data, our method achieved high fidelity for both trained (34.82 dB) and unseen (33.08 dB) directions. Most importantly, the synthesized angular data also improved the quantitative accuracy of downstream DTI model fitting. Our SA-INR framework breaks the classical sampling limits, paving the way for fast, quantitative high-resolution dMRI.

</details>


### [133] [Hyp2Former: Hierarchy-Aware Hyperbolic Embeddings for Open-Set Panoptic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02580)
*Yao Lu,Rohit Mohan,Florian Drews,Yakov Miron,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Hyp2Former提出了一种基于双曲空间的端到端开放集全景分割框架，通过编码已知类别的层次语义相似性，在不显式建模未知类的情况下，有效检测未知物体，并在多个数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放集全景分割方法将已知类别视为扁平标签集，忽略了语义层次结构所提供的结构性先验，这限制了对未知物体的有效识别，尤其对自动驾驶等安全关键应用至关重要。

Method: 提出Hyp2Former端到端框架，在双曲空间中连续学习已知类别的层次语义相似性，显式编码类别间的层级关系，使模型获得结构化的嵌入空间，未知物体因更靠近高层语义概念（如"动物"而非"电子产品"）而被可靠检测。

Result: 在MS COCO、Cityscapes、Lost&Found等多个公开数据集上，Hyp2Former在未知物体发现与分布内鲁棒性之间取得了最佳平衡，性能优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过利用双曲空间建模语义层次结构，无需在训练阶段显式建模未知类即可有效检测未知物体，为开放集全景分割提供了一种新范式。

Abstract: Recognizing unknown objects is crucial for safety-critical applications such as autonomous driving and robotics. Open-Set Panoptic Segmentation (OPS) aims to segment known thing and stuff classes while identifying valid unknown objects as separate instances. Prior OPS approaches largely treat known categories as a flat label set, ignoring the semantic hierarchy that provides valuable structural priors for distinguishing unknown objects from in-distribution classes. In this work, we propose Hyp2Former, an end-to-end framework for OPS that does not require explicit modeling of unknowns during training, and instead learns hierarchical semantic similarities continuously in hyperbolic space. By explicitly encoding hierarchical relationships among known categories, the model learns a structured embedding space that captures multiple levels of semantic abstraction. As a result, unknown objects that cannot be confidently classified as known categories still remain in close proximity to higher-level concepts (e.g., an unknown animal remains closer to "animal" or "object" than to unrelated concepts such as "electronics" or "stuff") and can therefore be reliably detected, even if their fine-grained category was not represented during training. Empirical evaluations across multiple public datasets such as MS COCO, Cityscapes, and Lost&Found demonstrate that Hyp2Former outperforms existing methods on OPS, achieving the best balance between unknown object discovery and in-distribution robustness.

</details>


### [134] [Stylistic Attribute Control in Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02583)
*Max Reimann,Benito Buchheim,Jürgen Döllner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过从合成数据集中学习解耦的编辑方向，实现对潜在扩散模型中风格属性的细粒度参数化控制的方法，利用引导组合缩小风格微调模型与基础模型之间的域差距，并引入训练正则化损失和优化的空条件嵌入以保持语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型在图像合成和编辑中取得了突破，但对风格属性的精确控制仍然具有挑战性，常常导致意外的内容修改。

Method: 1. 从合成数据集中学习解耦的编辑方向；2. 使用引导组合（guidance composition）来缩小风格微调模型与基础模型之间的域差距；3. 引入训练正则化损失（training regularization loss）；4. 增强DDIM反转，使用优化的空条件嵌入（null-conditional embeddings）进行真实图像编辑。

Result: 方法在轮廓、局部对比度、水彩化效果和几何图案等多种风格属性上进行了验证，相比现有的基于文本的编辑技术，提供了更精确、可连续调节且集成度更高的风格修改。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够实现精确、连续可调的风格修改，同时保持原始图像的语义内容不受影响。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models have revolutionized image synthesis and editing, but precise control over stylistic attributes remains a challenge, often causing unintended content modifications. We propose an approach for fine-grained parametric control of stylistic attributes in latent diffusion models by learning disentangled editing directions from synthetic datasets. We use guidance composition to close the domain gap between stylistically finetuned and foundation models, preserving the original image semantics while applying stylistic adjustments. To ensure consistent edits, we introduce a training regularization loss and enhance DDIM inversion with optimized null-conditional embeddings for real image editing. We validate our approach by learning from stylistically filtered synthetic datasets varying a range of stylistic attributes, including outlines, local contrast, watercolorization effects, and geometric patterns. Our evaluations demonstrate that compared to current text-based editing techniques, our method offers well-integrated, more precise and continuously adjustable stylistic modifications.

</details>


### [135] [StableMind: Source-Free Cross-Subject fMRI Decoding with Regularized Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02586)
*Jintao Guo,Lin Wang,Shumeng Li,Jian Zhang,Yulin Zhou,Luyang Cao,Hairong Zheng,Yinghuan Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StableMind提出了一种正则化适应框架，通过脑侧稳定性增强（岭投影先验+傅里叶增强）和图像侧监督可靠性提升（难度感知模糊对齐），在仅1小时新被试fMRI数据下实现跨被试解码，脑检索准确率超越SOTA 5.71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨被试fMRI解码方法在新被试数据有限且无法访问原始训练数据时性能显著下降，作者识别出两个关键问题：脑侧不稳定（被试间fMRI响应差异大）和图像侧监督不可靠（有限fMRI信号无法可靠支持细粒度视觉细节）。

Method: (1) 利用预训练模型的岭投影作为适应先验约束新被试适应，并引入傅里叶级特征级脑增强提升对个体差异的鲁棒性；(2) 提出难度感知图像模糊，减少有限fMRI信号难以支撑的细粒度视觉细节影响，同时保留稳定视觉结构。

Result: 在Natural Scenes Dataset上采用统一的1小时适应协议，四个被试平均图像检索准确率84.02%，脑检索准确率81.66%，脑检索准确率超越SOTA方法5.71%，且适应参数量更少。

Conclusion: StableMind通过正则化约束和数据增强有效解决了数据有限场景下跨被试fMRI解码的脑侧不稳定和图像侧监督不可靠问题，验证了方法的有效性和效率。

Abstract: Existing cross-subject fMRI decoding methods typically train a model on multiple scanned subjects and then adapt it to a new subject using substantial paired fMRI-image data. However, in realistic scenarios, new-subject fMRI data are often limited due to costly data acquisition, and raw data from previous subjects may be inaccessible, leading existing methods to suffer performance degradation during new-subject adaptation. In this paper, we identify that this degradation stems from two key issues: brain-side instability caused by large subject differences in fMRI responses, and image-side supervision unreliability caused by fine-grained visual details that are not reliably supported by limited fMRI signals. To address these challenges, we propose StableMind, a regularized adaptation framework designed to improve brain-side representation stability and image-side supervision reliability. (1) To stabilize brain representations, StableMind reuses ridge projections from the pretrained model as adaptation priors to constrain limited-data new-subject adaptation, and applies Fourier-based feature-level brain augmentation to improve robustness to individual variability. (2) To improve image supervision reliability, StableMind introduces difficulty-aware image blur for brain-image alignment, reducing the influence of fine-grained visual details that are weakly supported by limited fMRI signals while preserving stable visual structure. Experiments on the Natural Scenes Dataset under a unified 1-hour adaptation protocol demonstrate that StableMind achieves 84.02% image retrieval accuracy and 81.66% brain retrieval accuracy averaged over four subjects, surpassing the state-of-the-art method by 5.71% brain retrieval accuracy with fewer trainable adaptation parameters. Our code is available at https://github.com/lingeringlight/StableMind.

</details>


### [136] [Representation learning from OCT images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02589)
*Hedi Tabia,Désiré Sidibé,Nawres Khlifa,Ahmed Tabia,Ines Rahmany,Noura Aboudi,Zainab Haddad,Hajer Khachnaoui,Hsouna Zgolli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了视网膜OCT图像分析中的表示学习方法，涵盖从早期深度学习到最新基础模型与视觉语言系统的发展，按监督学习、自监督/半监督、生成方法、3D建模、多模态学习及大规模预训练基础模型等范式进行分类，并讨论了公开数据集、评估协议及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCT已成为眼科主要成像方式，但临床需求驱动下需处理大量采集数据，减少对专家标注的依赖并提升跨设备、跨人群的诊断一致性，因此表示学习成为核心研究方向。

Method: 该综述按照学习范式构建分类体系，系统分析了各范式下的核心方法贡献（包括CNN/Transformer监督学习、自监督/半监督、生成模型、3D体积建模、多模态表示学习及大规模预训练基础模型），并建立了统一的数学框架进行形式化描述。

Result: 提供了OCT公开数据集的系统梳理、评估协议讨论，识别了各范式间的演进脉络及持续存在的局限性，并归纳了当前最迫切的开放研究方向。

Conclusion: 指出了体素级基础模型预训练、不确定性感知表示学习、联邦学习与隐私保护训练、公平性与偏差缓解、基于概念的模型可解释性等关键未来研究方向。

Abstract: Optical Coherence Tomography (OCT) has become one of the most used imaging modality in ophthalmology. It provides high-resolution, non-invasive visualization of retinal microarchitecture. The automated analysis of OCT images through representation learning has emerged as a central research frontier. This has mainly been driven by the clinical need to process large acquisition volumes. The objective is to reduce the reliance on expert annotation, and improve diagnostic consistency across devices and populations. This survey provides a comprehensive and structured review of representation learning methods for retinal OCT image analysis. It covers the period from early deep learning approaches to the most recent developments in foundation models and vision-language systems. We organize the literature along a principled taxonomy of learning paradigms, encompassing supervised learning with CNN-based and transformer-based architectures, self-supervised and semi-supervised methods, generative approaches, as well as 3D volumetric modeling, multimodal representation learning, and large-scale pretrained foundation models. For each paradigm, we analyze the core methodological contributions, identify persistent limitations, and trace the connections between successive approaches. We further provide a structured overview of publicly available OCT datasets, discuss evaluation protocol considerations, and present a unified problem formulation that situates each learning paradigm within a common mathematical framework. Building on this analysis, we identify and discuss the most pressing open research directions emerging in the literature. This includes volumetric foundation model pretraining, uncertainty-aware representation learning, federated and privacy-preserving training, fairness and bias mitigation, concept-based interpretability,...

</details>


### [137] [Rethinking the Need for Source Models: Source-Free Domain Adaptation from Scratch Guided by a Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02604)
*Zhou Bingtao,Xiang Mian,Ning Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VODA（仅视觉-语言模型领域适应）这一更严格的设置，完全消除对源模型的依赖，仅使用随机初始化模型、ViL模型和无标签目标数据，并引入两阶段去噪区域蒸馏（TS-DRD）框架，在多个基准上达到甚至超越传统SFDA方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SFDA方法仍依赖源预训练模型初始化，并非真正无源；同时观察到不同源模型对同一目标域的最终结果影响很小，表明源模型的贡献有限，因此提出完全消除源依赖的VODA设置。

Method: 提出两阶段去噪区域蒸馏（TS-DRD）框架：第一阶段使用ViL模型对随机初始化模型进行预热；第二阶段寻找ViL模型与适应模型之间共存的去噪区域，生成更干净的蒸馏监督信号。

Result: 在Office-Home、VisDA和DomainNet-126数据集上，VODA设置下的TS-DRD方法取得了与甚至超越现有仍使用源模型的SFDA方法的性能。

Conclusion: VODA设置可行且有效，TS-DRD能够在不依赖任何源域信息的情况下实现优异的领域适应性能，为未来真正无源的领域适应研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Source-Free Domain Adaptation (SFDA) adapts source models to target domains without accessing source data, addressing privacy and transmission issues. However, existing methods still initialize from a source pre-trained model and thus are not truly source-free. Recent works have introduced Vision-Language (ViL) models to guide the adaptation process, in these methods, we observe that for the same target domain, different source models yield minimal variation in final results, indicating the source model itself has limited impact. Motivated by this, we propose ViL-Only Domain Adaptation (VODA) , a stricter setting that eliminates all dependencies on source domain, relying solely on a randomly initialized model, a ViL model, and unlabeled target data. We analyze the adaptation dynamics of VODA and introduce Two-Stage Denoised-Region Distillation (TS-DRD) , a two-stage framework that first warms up the model with ViL guidance, then seek a Denoised-Region inherent in both the ViL and adapting model, yielding cleaner supervision for distillation. Experiments on Office-Home, VisDA, and DomainNet-126 show that under VODA, TS-DRD achieves competitive or superior performance to existing SFDA methods that still use source models, demonstrating its effectiveness and the potential of the VODA setting.

</details>


### [138] [Validation of an AI-based end-to-end model for prostate pathology using long-term archived routine samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02614)
*Xiaoyi Ji,Renata Zelic,Oskar Aspegren,Nita Mulliqi,Michelangelo Fiorentino,Francesca Giunchi,Luca Molinaro,Sol Erika Boman,Lorenzo Richiardi,Andreas Pettersson,Per Henrik Vincent,Martin Eklund,Olof Akre,Kimmo Kartasalo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了GleasonAI模型在10,366个前列腺活检核心上的表现，该模型使用1998-2015年间瑞典14个地区的存档标本，在ISUP分级上达到0.86的二次加权kappa值，性能在17年间保持稳定，且AI分配的风险分组与前列腺癌特异性死亡率存在显著预后梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能在前列腺病理中正成为临床工具，但其在不同样本制备和长期保存条件下的泛化能力尚不明确，本研究旨在评估AI模型在时间和地域变化下的鲁棒性。

Method: 使用端到端注意力多实例学习模型GleasonAI，在包含1,028名患者、10,366个活检核心的独立验证队列上进行评估，数据来自瑞典14个地区、1998-2015年间收集的ProMort队列存档诊断标本。

Result: 核心级ISUP分级的二次加权kappa值为0.86，与多位经验丰富的病理学家相当；性能在14个地区和17年收集期内保持稳定；基于AI分级的风险组与前列腺癌特异性死亡率存在显著预后梯度。

Conclusion: AI分级模型具有良好的泛化性，病理档案可作为AI开发、验证和回顾性预后研究的大规模资源。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is becoming a clinical tool for prostate pathology, but generalization across variations in sample preparation and preservation over prolonged time periods remains poorly understood. We evaluated GleasonAI, an end-to-end attention-based multiple instance learning model, on an independent validation cohort comprising 10,366 biopsy cores from 1,028 patients across 14 Swedish regions, using archival diagnostic specimens from the ProMort cohorts collected between 1998-2015. The model achieved an overall quadratic-weighted kappa of 0.86 for core-level ISUP grading, comparable to several experienced pathologists and consistent across geographic regions. Notably, performance remained stable across the 17-year collection period, demonstrating robustness to time-related variation in archival material, a property not consistently observed with foundation model-based approaches, with exploratory analysis demonstrating a significant prognostic gradient across AI-assigned grade groups for prostate cancer-specific mortality. These findings support the generalizability of the AI grading model and demonstrate the potential of pathology archives as a large-scale resource for AI development, validation, and retrospective prognostic research.

</details>


### [139] [Global-Local Feature Decoding with Adapter-Guided SAMv2 for Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02616)
*Morteza Moradi,Mohammad Moradi,Simone Palazzo,Ali Borji,Concetto Spampinato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GLASSNet，利用冻结的SAMv2编码器（仅更新不足3%参数）结合轻量卷积适配器，通过全局-局部双解码器架构实现精准显著性目标检测，在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型（如SAM）在显著性目标检测（SOD）任务中潜力未被充分挖掘，且全参数微调计算成本高、在小数据下易过拟合。因此需要一种高效、轻量的方式来适配冻结的视觉基础模型以提升SOD性能。

Method: 采用SAMv2作为冻结编码器，搭配轻量级空间感知卷积适配器（可学习参数减少97%以上）；设计双解码器架构——一个解码器捕获全局长程语义（大感受野），另一个解码器聚焦边缘和纹理等局部细节，融合两者互补信息生成高质量显著图。

Result: 在标准SOD和伪装目标检测基准上，GLASSNet均超越现有最先进方法，证明了冻结基础模型结合定向适应与全局-局部解码的有效性。

Conclusion: 冻结大模型配合轻量适配器和全局-局部特征解码是一种高效且有效的SOD范式，可大幅降低计算开销的同时保持甚至提升性能。

Abstract: Salient Object Detection (SOD) remains an essential yet underexplored task in the era of large-scale vision models. Although foundation models like SAM exhibit strong generalization, their potential for SOD is not fully realized, and training or fully fine-tuning them is computationally expensive and prone to overfitting under limited data. To overcome these challenges, we introduce GLASSNet, a Global-Local feature decoding framework that uses SAMv2 as a frozen encoder paired with a lightweight, spatially aware convolutional adapter-reducing learnable encoder parameters by over 97%. To enhance saliency quality, GLASSNet employs a dual-decoder architecture: one decoder captures global, long-range semantics with an expanded receptive field, while the other captures fine local details such as edges and textures. Fusing these complementary cues yields saliency maps that combine global coherence with local precision, producing accurate final masks. Extensive experiments on standard SOD and camouflaged object detection benchmarks show that GLASSNet surpasses state-of-the-art methods, demonstrating the power of frozen foundation models combined with targeted adaptation and global-local decoding.

</details>


### [140] [Retrieving Any Relevant Moments: Benchmark and Models for Generalized Moment Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02623)
*Yiming Ding,Siyu Cao,Luyuan Jiao,Yixuan Li,Zitong Wang,Zhiyong Liu,Lu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出广义时刻检索（GMR）任务，用于处理视频中查询可能对应多个或零个时刻的真实场景，并构建了大规模基准 Soccer-GMR，设计了统一评估协议和基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频时刻检索（VMR）假设每个查询仅对应单个匹配时刻，但真实场景中查询可能对应多个时刻或没有匹配时刻，因此需要建立统一的广义时刻检索（GMR）框架。

Method: 1) 构建 Soccer-GMR 基准，采用半自动流水线（时长灵活+人工验证）进行可扩展数据生成；2) 设计统一评估协议，包含空集拒绝、正查询定位和端到端 GMR 性能的互补指标；3) 提出两种建模范式：轻量级即插即用 GMR 适配器（用于判别式 VMR 模型）和 GMR 定制的 GRPO 奖励（用于微调多模态大语言模型）。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提方法在所有指标上均取得一致提升，并揭示了现有方法的局限性。

Conclusion: GMR 是一个更真实且更具挑战性的视频-语言理解基准，所提基线方法有效且揭示了当前方法的不足。

Abstract: Video Moment Retrieval (VMR) aims to localize temporal segments in videos that correspond to a natural language query, but typically assumes only a single matching moment for each query. This assumption does not always hold in real-world scenarios, where queries may correspond to multiple or no moments. Thus, we formulate Generalized Moment Retrieval (GMR), a unified setting that requires retrieving the complete set of relevant moments or predicting an empty set. To enable systematic study of GMR, we introduce Soccer-GMR, a large-scale benchmark built on challenging soccer videos that reflect general GMR scenarios, with realistic negative and positive queries. The benchmark is constructed via a duration-flexible semi-automated pipeline with human verification, enabling scalable data generation while maintaining high annotation quality. We further design a unified evaluation protocol with complementary metrics tailored for null-set rejection, positive-query localization, and end-to-end GMR performance. Finally, we establish strong baselines across two modeling paradigms: a lightweight plug-and-play GMR adapter for discriminative VMR models, and a GMR-tailored GRPO reward for fine-tuning multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Extensive experiments show consistent gains across all metrics and expose key limitations of current methods, positioning GMR as a more realistic and challenging benchmark for video-language understanding.

</details>


### [141] [Rethinking Low-Light Image Enhancement: A Log-Domain Intensity--Chromaticity Decoupling Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02627)
*Guangrui Bai,Yifan Mei,Yahui Deng,Yuhan Chen,Yuze Qiu,Wenhai Liu,Erbao Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于解耦表示的显式重建约束方法，用于抑制低光图像增强中的异常通道放大和色噪问题，在多个基准数据集上达到了竞争性或更优的性能（LOLv2-Real上PSNR 29.71 dB, SSIM 0.89），并在DarkFace上验证了其对下游人脸检测任务的改善效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光图像增强中，现有方法容易出现异常通道放大和色度噪声问题，影响增强图像的视觉质量和后续任务性能。

Method: 从解耦表示中推导显式重建约束，对增强过程施加正则化，以抑制异常通道放大和色度噪声。

Result: 在LOLv2-Real上达到29.71 dB PSNR和0.89 SSIM；在MIT-Adobe FiveK和LSRW上也取得竞争性或更优的定量与视觉结果；DarkFace实验表明能改善低光条件下的下游人脸检测性能。

Conclusion: 所提显式重建约束方法能有效提升低光图像增强质量，并有利于下游视觉任务。

Abstract: Explicit reconstruction constraints derived from the decoupled representation are further imposed to suppress abnormal channel amplification and chromatic noise. Experiments on LOLv2-Real, MIT-Adobe FiveK, and LSRW show that the proposed method achieves competitive or superior quantitative and visual performance, reaching 29.71 dB PSNR and 0.89 SSIM on LOLv2-Real. DarkFace experiments further indicate improved downstream face detection under low-light conditions. Code and pretrained models are available at: https://github.com/mubaisam/ICD.

</details>


### [142] [AutoFocus: Uncertainty-Aware Active Visual Search for GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02630)
*Ruilin Yao,Shegnwu Xiong,Tianyu Zou,Shili Xiong,Yi Rong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AutoFocus是一种无需训练、基于不确定性感知的主动视觉搜索框架，通过token级困惑度构建各向异性高斯空间概率场，自适应生成缩放区域，解决高分辨率界面中GUI视觉定位的性能下降问题，在ScreenSpot-Pro和ScreenSpot-V2上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在GUI定位中分辨率不足，高分辨率界面下密集布局和小组件导致性能下降；现有缩放策略依赖固定锚点、启发式网格或强化学习，缺乏自适应确定需要精炼区域和探索空间不确定性的机制。

Method: 1) 多次采样坐标假设，将轴向困惑度转换为各向异性高斯空间概率场以建模方向不确定性；2) 基于该场生成全局和局部区域提议；3) 引入Shape-Aware Zooming平衡紧密定位与上下文保留；4) 通过视觉提示聚合步骤进行结构化比较选择最一致的预测。

Result: 在ScreenSpot-Pro和ScreenSpot-V2基准上，AutoFocus在通用VLM和GUI专用VLM上均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: AutoFocus通过不确定性感知的主动视觉搜索，无需训练即可自适应确定精炼区域，有效弥合了高分辨率界面与模型输入约束之间的分辨率差距，提升了GUI grounding的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have enabled autonomous GUI agents that translate natural language instructions into executable screen coordinates. However, grounding performance degrades in high-resolution interfaces, where dense layouts and small interactive elements expose a resolution gap between modern displays and model input constraints. Existing zoom-in strategies rely on fixed anchors, heuristic grids, or reinforcement learning, lacking a principled mechanism to adaptively determine where refinement is needed and how much spatial uncertainty should be explored. We propose AutoFocus, a training-free, uncertainty-aware active visual search framework for GUI grounding. Our key insight is that token-level perplexity in coordinate generation naturally reflects spatial uncertainty. Rather than committing to a single prediction, AutoFocus samples multiple coordinate hypotheses and converts their axial perplexities into an anisotropic gaussian spatial probability field, explicitly modeling directional uncertainty. Based on this field, we generate global and local region proposals and introduce Shape-Aware Zooming to balance tight localization with contextual preservation. A visual prompt-based aggregation step then selects the most consistent prediction via structured comparison. Extensive experiments on ScreenSpot-Pro and ScreenSpot-V2 demonstrate consistent improvements across both general-purpose and GUI-specialized VLMs.

</details>


### [143] [ViewSAM: Learning View-aware Cross-modal Semantics for Weakly Supervised Cross-view Referring Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02638)
*Jiawei Ge,Xintian Zhang,Jiuxin Cao,Bo Liu,Fabian Deuser,Chang Liu,Gong Wenkang,Siyou Li,Juexi Shao,Wenqing Wu,Chen Feng,Ioannis Patras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种弱监督的跨视角指代多目标跟踪（CRMOT）框架，利用SAM2/SAM3等基础模型生成伪标签，仅在物体类别标签监督下，通过两阶段训练（跨视角重提示+视图感知模型ViewSAM）实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CRMOT方法严重依赖昂贵的帧级空间标注和跨视角身份监督，为降低这种人工标注成本，本文探索在弱监督条件下利用基础模型的能力。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段设计Affinity-guided Cross-view Re-prompting策略，精炼并关联SAM3生成的跨视角轨迹，产生可靠的伪标签；第二阶段引入ViewSAM模型，基于SAM2构建，通过将视角变化建模为可学习条件，弥合视角变化视觉观察与视角不变文本表达之间的差距。

Result: ViewSAM在弱监督条件下达到SOTA性能，且与全监督方法性能相当；仅增加约10%的参数量即可实现鲁棒的跨视角指代跟踪。

Conclusion: 通过将基础模型重新用作伪标签生成器，并设计视图感知的跨模态建模，可以在大幅降低标注依赖的同时实现与全监督方法可比的CRMOT性能。

Abstract: Cross-view Referring Multi-Object Tracking (CRMOT) aims to track multiple objects specified by natural language across multiple camera views, with globally consistent identities. Despite recent progress, existing methods rely heavily on costly frame-level spatial annotations and cross-view identity supervision. To reduce such reliance, we explore CRMOT under weak supervision by leveraging the capabilities of foundation models. However, our empirical study shows that directly applying foundation models such as SAM2 and SAM3, even with task-specific modifications, fails to accurately understand referring expressions and maintain consistent identities across views. Yet, they remain effective at producing reliable object tracklets that can serve as pseudo supervision. We therefore repurpose foundation models as pseudo-label generators and propose a two-stage framework for weakly supervised CRMOT, using only object category labels as coarse-grained supervision. In the first stage, we design an Affinity-guided Cross-view Re-prompting strategy to refine and associate SAM3-generated tracklets across cameras, producing reliable cross-view pseudo labels for subsequent training. In the second stage, we introduce ViewSAM, a CRMOT model built upon SAM2 that explicitly models view-aware cross-modal semantics. By formulating view-induced variations as learnable conditions, ViewSAM bridges the gap between view-variant visual observations and view-invariant textual expressions, enabling robust cross-view referring tracking with only approximately 10% additional parameters. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ViewSAM achieves SOTA performance under weak supervision and remains competitive with fully supervised methods.

</details>


### [144] [Mamoda2.5: Enhancing Unified Multimodal Model with DiT-MoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02641)
*Yangming Shi,Shixiang Zhu,Tao Shen,Zhimiao Yu,Dengsheng Chen,Taicai Chen,Yunfei Yang,Juan Zhou,Chen Cheng,Liang Ma,Xibin Wu,Benxuan Yan,Ge Li,Tuoyu Zhang,Dan Li,Chang Liu,Zhenbang Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mamoda2.5是一个统一AR-Diffusion框架，使用MoE设计（128专家，Top-8路由）的25B参数模型（仅激活3B），在VBench 2.0和视频编辑任务上达到顶尖水平，并通过蒸馏和强化学习将30步编辑模型压缩为4步，实现高达95.9倍推理加速，在广告场景中成功部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在多模态理解与生成上分离，缺乏统一的单架构方案，且生成能力与推理效率之间存在权衡，亟需一种既能扩展模型容量又能降低训练和推理成本的方法。

Method: 基于Diffusion Transformer骨干，引入细粒度MoE设计（128个专家，Top-8路由），构建25B参数模型（激活3B）；提出联合几步蒸馏与强化学习框架，将30步编辑模型压缩为4步。

Result: 在VBench 2.0上取得顶尖生成性能；在OpenVE-Bench视频编辑质量上超越开源模型，媲美Kling O1等顶级闭源模型；相比开源基线实现最高95.9倍视频编辑推理加速；在广告视频编辑场景中达到98%成功率。

Conclusion: Mamoda2.5通过统一的AR-Diffusion架构和MoE设计，在保证高效训练和推理的同时实现了多模态理解与生成的一体化，证明了稀疏激活大模型在多模态任务上的巨大潜力。

Abstract: We present Mamoda2.5, a unified AR-Diffusion framework that seamlessly integrates multimodal understanding and generation within a single architecture. To efficiently enhance the model's generation capability, we equip the Diffusion Transformer backbone with a fine-grained Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) design (128 experts, Top-8 routing), yielding a 25B-parameter model that activates only 3B parameters, significantly reducing training costs while scaling up the model capacity. Mamoda2.5 achieves top-tier generation performance on VBench 2.0 and sets a new record in video editing quality, surpassing evaluated open-source models and matching the performance of current top-tier proprietary models, including the Kling O1 on OpenVE-Bench. Furthermore, we introduce a joint few-step distillation and reinforcement learning framework that compresses the 30-step editing model into a 4-step model and greatly accelerates model inference. Compared to open-source baselines, Mamoda2.5 achieves up to $95.9\times$ faster video editing inference. In real-world applications, Mamoda2.5 has been successfully deployed for content moderation and creative restoration tasks in advertising scenarios, achieving a 98% success rate in internal advertising video editing scenario.

</details>


### [145] [Human Activity Recognition Method for Moderate Violence Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02659)
*Luis Angel Aparicio Borjas,Victor Elias Nieto,Juan Irving Vasquez,Alfonso Fernandez-Vazquez,Gerardo Antonio Alvarez Hernandez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基于YOLO11、YOLO11-Pose和随机森林分类器的自动检测系统，通过分析人体骨骼关键点（身体倾斜角度、肩髋关节角度）实时识别监控视频中的推搡行为。在受控正面视角下精确率达0.98，在真实监控场景（高海拔、陡角度）下精确率仍保持0.72，证明了利用骨骼分析进行早期暴力干预的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共场所的身体暴力是重要的公共卫生问题，轻微的推搡行为往往是更严重暴力升级的前兆。现有监控系统缺乏对中等程度暴力（如推搡）的实时自动检测能力，因此需要开发一种自动化系统来实现早期干预。

Method: 集成YOLO11和YOLO11-Pose进行人体检测和骨骼关键点提取，计算身体倾斜角度以及肩部和臀部之间的关节角度，使用随机森林分类器区分正常行为和攻击性身体接触。通过三个难度递增的案例研究评估系统性能。

Result: 在受控正面视角环境下精确率达0.98；在最具挑战性的真实监控场景（高海拔、陡角度、存在视角畸变和视觉噪声）中精确率保持0.72。

Conclusion: 利用骨骼分析进行早期暴力干预在城市场景中是可行的，即使在存在显著视角畸变和噪声的真实监控条件下仍能保持有效的检测性能。

Abstract: Physical violence in public spaces is a significant public health concern, with minor incidents such as pushing often serving as precursors to more severe escalations. This research develops an automated system for the real-time detection of moderate physical violence, specifically pushing, in surveillance camera footage. The proposed solution integrates state-of-the-art computer vision models, utilizing YOLO11 and YOLO11-Pose for human detection and skeletal keypoint extraction. By calculating body inclination and joint angles between shoulders and hips, a Random Forest classifier was trained to distinguish between normal behavior and aggressive physical contact. The system's performance was evaluated through three progressive case studies representing increasing levels of difficulty. In controlled environments with frontal views, the model achieved a precision of 0.98.
