Motivation: 在线广告治理面临监管政策非平稳变化的挑战：新出台的监管要求（如教育或容貌焦虑限制）与历史数据集中的旧标签产生严重不一致，导致推理歧义。现有方法难以适应这种动态演化的政策环境。

Method: 提出ARGUS三阶段框架：(1)策略种子初始化感知；(2)对抗性标签修正，采用"检察官-辩护人-裁判"架构解决旧标签与新政策的冲突；(3)隐知识发现，通过三方辩证讨论挖掘灰色地带违规行为。利用RAG增强的策略知识和思维链合成作为强化学习的动态奖励，使推理路径与政策演变同步。

Result: 在工业数据集和公开数据集上的实验表明，ARGUS显著优于传统的微调基线方法，在极少金标准数据下实现了优异的策略自适应学习性能。

Conclusion: 多智能体对抗仲裁框架能有效解决政策非平稳变化下的标签不一致问题，RAG+CoT作为动态奖励的强化学习范式为策略自适应治理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Online advertising governance faces significant challenges due to the non-stationary nature of regulatory policies, where emerging mandates (e.g., restrictions on education or aesthetic anxiety) create severe label inconsistencies and reasoning ambiguities in historical datasets. In this paper, we propose ARGUS, a policy-adaptive governance system that enables evolving reinforcement through multi-agent adversarial umpiring. ARGUS addresses the sparsity of new policy data by employing a three-stage framework: (1) Policy Seeding for initial perception; (2) Adversarial Label Rectification, which utilizes a ``Prosecutor-Defender-Umpire'' architecture to resolve conflicts between stale labels and new mandates; and (3) Latent Knowledge Discovery, which employs a tripartite dialectical discussion to unearth sophisticated, ``gray-area'' violations. By leveraging RAG-enhanced policy knowledge and Chain-of-Thought synthesis as dynamic rewards for reinforcement learning, ARGUS synchronizes its reasoning pathways with evolving regulations. Extensive experiments on both industrial and public datasets demonstrate that ARGUS significantly outperforms traditional fine-tuning baselines, achieving superior policy-adaptive learning with minimal gold data.

</details>


### [221] [An Information-theoretic Propagation Denoising and Fusion Framework for Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02259)
*Mengyang Chen,Lingwei Wei,Wei Zhou,Songlin Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出InfoPDF框架，从互信息角度缓解合成传播数据的不可靠性，通过将合成传播图建模为概率潜分布、设计互信息损失函数来学习压缩且任务充分的传播表征，在三个数据集上实现了优越的假新闻检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不完整的传播数据严重阻碍了鲁棒的假新闻检测。现有方法利用大语言模型通过角色扮演模拟缺失的用户交互，但合成的传播数据本质上不可靠，直接融合会导致有偏的表征，限制检测性能。

Method: 首先利用大语言模型生成属性特定的合成传播数据；然后将每个合成传播图建模为概率潜分布，以引导与真实传播的自适应融合；最后设计基于互信息的目标函数，联合抑制合成传播中的噪声信号、保持真实与合成传播表征的一致性、确保假新闻检测和属性预测的任务充分性。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上，InfoPDF在各种假新闻检测任务中一致取得优越性能。进一步分析表明，InfoPDF能够估计属性级可靠性并学习更具判别性的传播表征。

Conclusion: 从互信息视角缓解合成传播不可靠性的思路是有效的，InfoPDF框架能够学习更鲁棒且判别性的传播表征以提升假新闻检测性能。

Abstract: Incomplete propagation data significantly hinders robust fake news detection. Recent approaches leverage large language models to simulate missing user interactions via role-playing, thereby enriching propagation with synthetic signals. However, such propagation data is intrinsically unreliable, and directly fusing it can lead to biased representations, leading to limited detection performance. In this paper, we alleviate the unreliability of synthetic propagation from the mutual information perspective and propose a novel information-theoretic propagation denoising and fusion (InfoPDF) framework to learn effective representations from both real and synthetic propagation. Specifically, we first generate attribute-specific synthetic propagation using large language models. Then we model each synthetic propagation graph as a probabilistic latent distribution to guide reliability-aware adaptive fusion with real propagation. During training, we design a mutual information-based objective to learn compressed and task-sufficient propagation representations. It jointly suppresses noisy signals across attribute-specific synthetic propagation, maintains consistency between real and synthetic propagation representations, and ensures task sufficiency for fake news detection and attribute prediction. Experiments on three real-world datasets show that InfoPDF consistently achieves superior performance across various fake news detection tasks. Further analysis demonstrates that InfoPDF can estimate attribute-level reliabilities and learn more discriminative propagation representations.

</details>


### [222] [Reliability-Oriented Multilingual Orthopedic Diagnosis: A Domain-Adaptive Modeling and a Conceptual Validation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02266)
*Danish Ali,Li Xiaojian,Sundas Iqbal,Farrukh Zaidi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了多语言骨科诊断中LLM的可靠性、校准和安全性，发现零样本LLM在低资源语言下校准不稳定、可靠性降低，而领域自适应架构（IndicBERT-HPA）显著提升了跨语言判别能力和置信度行为，并提出了确定性智能体验证框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM被越来越多地用于临床决策支持（包括低资源环境下的多语言诊断），但其在高风险结构化任务中的可靠性、校准和安全性特征尚未得到充分理解。

Method: 基于英语、印地语和旁遮普语的骨科自由文本临床笔记，对比三种建模范式：(i)任务对齐的多语言Transformer编码器、(ii)任务微调基线（DistilBERT）、(iii)领域自适应架构（IndicBERT-HPA），并与零样本指令微调LLM进行比较。

Result: LLM表现出语言流畅性，但在结构化多语言条件下校准不稳定、可靠性降低；领域自适应显著改善跨语言判别和置信度行为；IndicBERT-HPA在六个诊断类别上表现一致且部署特征更可预测。

Conclusion: 可靠的多语言临床决策支持需要专用架构、明确的可靠性分析和结构化验证；研究结果仅针对零样本评估，不暗示微调模型的局限性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly proposed for clinical decision support including multilingual diagnosis in low-resource settings. However, their reliability, calibration and safety characteristics remain insufficiently understood for structured, high-risk tasks. We present a system-level analysis of multilingual orthopedic diagnosis from free-text clinical notes in English, Hindi and Punjabi. We evaluate three modeling regimes: (i) task-aligned multilingual transformer encoders, (ii) a task-fine-tuned baseline (DistilBERT), and (iii) a domain-adaptive architecture tailored to orthopedic text (IndicBERT-HPA). These models are compared with zero-shot, instruction-tuned LLMs to assess suitability for structured diagnostic classification. Results indicate that while LLMs exhibit strong linguistic fluency, they show unstable calibration and reduced reliability under structured multilingual conditions, particularly in low-resource languages. These findings are specific to zero-shot evaluation and do not imply limitations of fine-tuned models. Domain-adaptive specialization substantially improves cross-lingual discrimination and confidence behavior. IndicBERT-HPA, with language-specific orthopedic adapter heads achieves consistently strong performance across six diagnostic categories and more predictable deployment characteristics than task-only adaptation. Building on these observations, we outline a conceptual deterministic agent-based validation framework for future implementation, formalizing evidence checks, language-sensitive validation and conservative human-in-the-loop gating. Reliable multilingual clinical decision support requires specialized architecture, explicit reliability analysis, and structured validation for safety-critical systems.

</details>


### [223] [A Systematic Benchmark of Machine Transliteration Models for the Tajik-Farsi Language Pair: A Comparative Study from Rule-Based to Transformer Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02270)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次全面比较了现代机器学习架构在塔吉克语（西里尔字母）和波斯语（阿拉伯字母）之间的音译任务，创建并验证了来自多源异构的平行语料库（最终使用40,000句对），实验对比了六类模型，结果显示字节级ByT5以chrF++ 87.4（塔→波）和80.1（反方向）大幅领先，字符级G2P Transformer（72.3）显著优于mBART（62.2），而基于子词分词的mT5完全失败（<18.5），结论表明字节/字符级架构对此音译任务远优于传统子词级多语言Seq2Seq模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 塔吉克语（西里尔文）与波斯语（阿拉伯文）之间的音译缺乏系统的数据资源和模型对比研究，现有多语言模型在音译任务上表现未知，亟需构建平行语料并系统评估不同架构对此特殊语言对的适用性。

Method: 构建了来自多源异构（众包、词典对、《列王纪》平行文本、外交文章、《玛斯纳维》、官方术语表、音译对应表）的平行语料，初始328,253句对，分层随机抽样形成40,000句对的代表性子集；对比六类模型：规则基线、LSTM+注意力、字符级Transformer、从零训练的G2P Transformer、预训练多语言模型（mBART、mT5+LoRA）和字节级ByT5，使用chrF++评估。

Result: ByT5全面最优（塔→波chrF++ 87.4，反方向80.1）；G2P Transformer（72.3）显著优于mBART（62.2）；基于子词分词的mT5完全失败（chrF++ < 18.5）；字符级和字节级模型整体优于子词级模型。

Conclusion: 对于塔吉克语-波斯语音译任务，字节级或字符级架构明确优于传统依赖子词分词的预训练多语言Seq2Seq模型，建议在实际应用中优先采用字节/字符级模型。

Abstract: This paper presents the first comprehensive comparative analysis of modern machine learning architectures for transliteration between Tajik (Cyrillic script) and Persian (Arabic script). A key contribution is the creation and validation of a unique parallel corpus aggregated from multiple heterogeneous sources, including crowdsourced projects, lexicographic pairs, parallel texts of "Shahnameh", diplomatic articles, texts of "Masnavi-i Ma'navi", official terminology lists, and transliterated correspondences. The initial dataset comprised 328,253 sentence pairs; a representative subset of 40,000 pairs was formed using stratified random sampling.
  The experiment compared six classes of models: rule-based baseline, LSTM with attention, character-level Transformer, G2P Transformer (trained from scratch), pre-trained multilingual models (mBART, mT5 with LoRA), and byte-level ByT5. Results demonstrate the overwhelming superiority of ByT5 (chrF++ 87.4 for Tajik to Farsi, 80.1 for reverse). The G2P Transformer significantly outperformed mBART (72.3 vs. 62.2 chrF++) despite limited data. Models using subword tokenization (mT5) failed completely (chrF++ less than 18.5).
  The findings demonstrate that for accurate transliteration of the Tajik-Farsi pair, architectures operating at the byte or character level are unequivocally more effective than traditional multilingual Seq2Seq models relying on subword tokenization.

</details>


### [224] [Compositional Multi-hop Factual Error Correction via Decomposition-and-Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02277)
*Lei Zhu,Xiaobao Wang,Jianbiao Yang,Chenyang Wang,Dongxiao He,Longbiao Wang,Jianwu Dang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CECoR框架，通过将多跳声明分解为可解释的推理步骤并注入受控扰动来生成高质量训练数据，结合监督微调与强化学习的两阶段策略，有效解决了多跳事实错误校正中的组合推理难题，在多跳和单跳基准上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事实错误校正方法主要针对单跳场景，将声明视为原子单元，难以处理需要跨多个证据源进行组合推理的多跳情况。同时，缺乏配对训练数据以及在复杂推理链中定位语义错误存在困难，这些问题进一步加剧了多跳校正的挑战。

Method: CECoR框架采用"分解与注入"范式：(1) 将多跳声明分解为可解释的推理步骤；(2) 在推理步骤中注入受控扰动以合成高质量训练数据；(3) 两阶段学习策略：先进行监督微调，再采用强化学习提升事实准确性和鲁棒性。

Result: 在多跳基准测试上，CECoR优于远监督方法和少样本大语言模型基线；在单跳校正任务上表现良好；在噪声证据环境下保持稳定性能，展现了较强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: CECoR通过推理感知的合成和两阶段学习，有效解决了多跳事实错误校正中的组合推理难题，其在多跳和单跳场景下的优异表现以及对抗噪声证据的稳定性，证明了该方法在现实事实校正任务中的实用性和通用性。

Abstract: Factual Error Correction (FEC) aims to revise inaccurate text into statements that are factually consistent with external evidence. Although recent methods perform well on single-hop correction, they often treat claims as atomic units and struggle with multi-hop cases that require compositional reasoning across multiple evidence sources. This challenge is further amplified by limited paired data and difficulties in locating semantic errors within complex reasoning chains. We present CECoR (Compositional Error Correction via Reasoning-aware Synthesis), a reasoning-aware framework that introduces a Decomposition and Injection paradigm for compositional error correction. CECoR decomposes multi-hop claims into interpretable reasoning steps and injects controlled perturbations to synthesize high-quality training pairs. A two-stage learning strategy combining supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning improves factual accuracy and robustness. Comprehensive evaluations show that CECoR achieves strong performance on multi-hop benchmarks, outperforming both distantly supervised methods and few-shot LLM baselines. It also generalizes effectively to single-hop correction and remains stable under noisy evidence, demonstrating its versatility for real-world factual correction.

</details>


### [225] [Structural Dilemmas and Developmental Pathways of Legal Argument Mining in the Era of Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02308)
*Xianglei Liao,Chuanyi Li,Kun Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统回顾了法律论证挖掘在数据、技术、理论三个维度的发展现状，指出其发展缓慢的根本原因在于缺乏一种能够兼顾理论表达力与计算可行性的结构化表示方法，并提出了未来研究的关键方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人工智能快速发展的背景下，法律论证挖掘作为连接法律文本与智能分析的重要研究领域具有重要的理论与现实意义，但该领域整体发展相对缓慢，需要深入分析其根本原因并指明未来方向。

Method: 基于对现有研究在数据（原始法律文本与标注语料库）、技术（从规则系统到传统机器学习再到LLM的范式演进）和理论（论证理论与法律教义学）三个维度的系统综述，进行深入分析。

Result: 发现发展缓慢的根本原因并非数据稀缺或技术限制，而在于缺乏兼顾理论表达力与计算可行性的结构化表示方法，具体表现为数据标准化困境、有效建模障碍和领域适应局限性。

Conclusion: 研究提出若干未来研究的关键方向，旨在为法律论证挖掘提供关键问题的重新框架和发展路径，具体模型与实现方案留待后续研究。

Abstract: Against the backdrop of rapid advances in artificial intelligence, legal argument mining has emerged as an important research area linking legal texts with intelligent analysis, carrying significant theoretical and practical implications. Existing studies have primarily developed along three dimensions: data, technology, and theory. At the data level, raw legal texts and annotated corpora constitute the foundational resources. At the technological level, research paradigms have evolved from rule-based systems and traditional machine learning to large language models (LLMs). At the theoretical level, argumentation theory and legal dogmatics provide important references for modeling argumentation structures. However, despite ongoing progress, the overall development of legal argument mining remains relatively slow. Building on a systematic review of existing research, this study conducts an in-depth analysis and finds that this is due not only to data scarcity or technical limitations, but more fundamentally to the lack of a structured representational approach that reconciles theoretical expressiveness with computational feasibility. Specifically, this challenge manifests in dilemmas in data standardization, obstacles to effective modeling, and limitations in domain adaptation. In response, the study proposes several key directions for future research. It aims to provide a reframing of key problems and a pathway for future development in legal argument mining, while leaving specific models and implementation schemes for further investigation.

</details>


### [226] [Decoding-Time Debiasing via Process Reward Models: From Controlled Fill-in to Open-Ended Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02348)
*Muneeb Ur Raheem Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种无需修改模型权重的解码时去偏方法，通过过程奖励模型(PRM)对候选token进行公平性和流畅性评分，设计了三种去偏方案，在4个模型和8类偏见上进行评估，顺序去偏效果最佳，可将偏见分数提升最多+0.40。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型从训练数据中习得社会偏见并在下游应用中强化刻板印象，现有修复方法（如重训练或微调）成本高、需访问模型权重且可能降低其他任务性能，因此需要一种轻量级、无需修改权重且不影响模型通用能力的去偏方法。

Method: 提出解码时去偏框架，核心是过程奖励模型(PRM)作为裁判对候选token进行公平性和流畅性评分。设计三种方案：Best-of-N选择、顺序批评-修订(Sequential critique-and-revise)和宪法自我审计(Constitutional self-audit)。在4个模型上使用200条双语（英语/乌尔都语）提示词进行8类偏见评估，并扩展到开放式生成（逐token实时去偏），引入轻量级Bias Guard门控机制仅对可能偏见的词触发。

Result: 顺序去偏最有效，偏见分数比基线提升最多+0.40，同时保持甚至提升流畅性。Best-of-N在原生实现中生成器侧开销几乎为零。GPT-4o-mini验证了框架随模型能力扩展，三个开源模型展示了小规模LLM仍面临的挑战。

Conclusion: 解码时去偏是现有方法的一种可行替代方案，能在不修改模型权重的情况下有效缓解偏见，顺序批评-修订策略在效果和流畅性之间取得了最佳平衡，但小规模开源模型仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Large language models pick up social biases from the data they are trained on and carry those biases into downstream applications, often reinforcing stereotypes around gender, race, religion, disability, age, and socioeconomic status. The standard fixes (retraining on curated data or fine-tuning with human feedback) are expensive, need access to model weights, and risk degrading the model on other tasks. In this paper we take a different route: we debias the model at decoding time, treating bias mitigation as a structured search over candidate tokens without ever touching model weights. A separate Process Reward Model (PRM) acts as a judge, scoring each candidate for both fairness and fluency. We design three schemes of increasing sophistication (Best-of-N selection, Sequential critique-and-revise, and Constitutional self-audit) and evaluate them on four models (GPT-4o-mini, Llama 3.2 3B, Gemma 3 4B, Qwen 2.5 3B) across a 200-prompt bilingual benchmark in English and Urdu covering eight bias categories. Sequential debiasing proves the most effective, raising mean bias scores by up to +0.40 over baseline while preserving (and sometimes improving) fluency. We then extend all three schemes to open-ended generation, where each token is debiased on the fly, and introduce a lightweight Bias Guard gate that fires only on potentially biased words, keeping overhead near 2x for well-calibrated models. A formal overhead metric that separates generator cost from judge cost reveals that Best-of-N is effectively free on the generator side in a native implementation. GPT-4o-mini, included as a strong proprietary anchor, confirms that the framework scales with model capability; the three open-weight models show where current small-scale LLMs still struggle.

</details>


### [227] [MolViBench: Evaluating LLMs on Molecular Vibe Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02351)
*Jiatong Li,Yuxuan Ren,Weida Wang,Changmeng Zheng,Xiao-yong Wei,Qing Li,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MolViBench基准，首个针对分子振动编码的评估框架，包含358个跨5个认知级别、覆盖12个药物发现流程的任务，以及多层代码评估体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准存在脱节：通用代码基准（如HumanEval）缺乏化学知识，化学基准（如S^2-Bench）评估知识回忆而非可执行代码生成，缺乏专门评估分子振动编码的基准。

Method: 构建358个跨5个认知级别的任务（从单API调用到端到端虚拟筛选），覆盖12个真实药物发现流程；提出多层评估框架，结合类型感知输出比较和AST-based API语义回退分析。

Result: 系统评估9个前沿编码LLM，比较三种真实分子振动编码范式，为诊断LLM在AI加速分子发现中的编码能力提供了实用且细粒度的测试平台。

Conclusion: MolViBench弥合了通用代码生成与化学知识之间的评估鸿沟，为分子振动编码提供了首个专用基准，揭示了当前LLM在分子编码任务上的能力与局限。

Abstract: Molecular Vibe Coding, a paradigm where chemists interact with LLMs to generate executable programs for molecular tasks, has emerged as a flexible alternative to chemical agents with predefined tools, enabling chemists to express arbitrarily complex, customized workflows. Unlike general coding tasks, molecular coding imposes a distinctive challenge that LLMs should jointly equip programming, molecular understanding, and domain-specific reasoning capabilities. However, existing benchmarks remain disconnected. General code generation benchmarks such as HumanEval and SWE-bench require no chemistry knowledge, while chemistry-focused benchmarks such as S^2-Bench and ChemCoTBench evaluate knowledge recall or property prediction rather than executable code generation. To bridge this gap, we introduce MolViBench, the first benchmark tailored for Molecular Vibe Coding. MolViBench comprises 358 curated tasks across five cognitive levels, ranging from single-API recall to end-to-end virtual screening pipeline design, spanning 12 real-world drug discovery workflows. To rigorously assess generated code, we also propose a multi-layered evaluation framework that combines type-aware output comparison and AST-based API-semantic fallback analysis, which jointly measures executability and chemical correctness. We systematically evaluate 9 frontier coding LLMs and compare three real-world Molecular Vibe Coding paradigms, providing a practical and fine-grained testbed for diagnosing LLMs' coding capabilities in AI-accelerated molecular discovery.

</details>


### [228] [InfoLaw: Information Scaling Laws for Large Language Models with Quality-Weighted Mixture Data and Repetition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02364)
*Fengze Liu,Weidong Zhou,Binbin Liu,Ping Guo,Zijun Wang,Bingni Zhang,Yifan Zhang,Yifeng Yu,Xiaohuan Zhou,Taifeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出InfoLaw（信息缩放定律），一种数据感知的缩放框架，通过将预训练建模为信息累积过程，预测损失与数据混合配方、重复次数和模型规模的关系，在0.15%平均绝对误差内预测未见数据配方，并可靠外推到过训练场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缩放定律在数据混合配方变化和重复训练条件下无法可靠外推，导致在有限数据场景下最优数据配方的选择是一个欠定问题。

Method: 将预训练建模为信息累积过程，其中数据质量控制信息密度，重复训练引入规模依赖的递减回报。收集不同规模、质量分布和重复水平的数据集训练结果，建立信息量模型以准确预测模型性能。

Result: 在高达7B参数、425B token的规模上，InfoLaw对未见数据配方的损失预测平均绝对误差为0.15%，最大绝对误差为0.96%，并能在不同过训练水平下可靠外推。

Conclusion: InfoLaw实现了在不同计算预算下高效的数据配方选择，解决了数据有限场景下高质量数据加权与重复训练之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Upweighting high-quality data in LLM pretraining often improves performance, but in datalimited regimes, especially under overtraining, stronger upweighting increases repetition and can degrade performance. However, standard scaling laws do not reliably extrapolate across mixture recipes or under repetitions, making the selection for optimal data recipes at scaling underdetermined. To solve this, we introduce InfoLaw (Information Scaling Laws), a data-aware scaling framework that predicts loss from consumed tokens, model size, data mixture weights, and repetition. The key idea is to model pretraining as information accumulation, where quality controls information density and repetition induces scaledependent diminishing returns. We first collect the model performance after training on datasets that vary in scale, quality distribution, and repetition level. Then we build up the modeling for information so that information accurately predicts those model performance. InfoLaw predicts performance on unseen data recipes and larger scale runs (up to 7B, 425B tokens) with 0.15% mean and 0.96% max absolute error in loss, and it extrapolates reliably across overtraining levels, enabling efficient data-recipe selection under varying compute budgets.

</details>


### [229] [Is It Novel and Why? Fine-Grained Patent Novelty Prediction Based on Passage Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02392)
*Valentin Knappich,Anna Hätty,Simon Razniewski,Annemarie Friedrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了FiNE-Patents数据集（3,658个专利首条权利要求），将专利新颖性评估从二元分类任务重新定义为细粒度的特征级联合检索与抽象推理任务，并证明LLM工作流在段落检索和新颖特征识别上优于基线方法，且对虚假关联具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的专利新颖性预测主要被当作权利要求级别的二元分类任务，但这种范式容易受到虚假关联的影响，且缺乏实际应用所需的细粒度。作者希望通过引入特征级别的细粒度标注和评估范式，提升新颖性评估的透明性和实用性。

Method: 构建FiNE-Patents数据集（从欧洲检索意见文档中提取3,658条首条权利要求及其特征级现有技术引用）；提出将任务重新定义为特征级的联合检索与抽象推理；设计LLM工作流：将权利要求分解为特征，逐特征与现有技术对比分析，最终得出权利要求级新颖性预测。

Result: LLM工作流在段落检索和新颖特征识别任务上优于基于嵌入的基线方法；与训练的分类器不同，LLM对权利要求级新颖性分类中的虚假关联表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 从二元分类转向细粒度特征级推理是提升专利新颖性评估的有效路径，LLM在此任务上展现了优势，数据集和代码已开源以促进进一步研究。

Abstract: Novelty assessment is a critical yet complex task in the examination process for patent acceptance, requiring examiners to determine whether an invention is disclosed in a prior art document. The process involves intricate matching between specific features of a patent claim and passages in the prior art. While prior work has approached novelty prediction primarily as a binary classification task at the claim level, we argue that this formulation is susceptible to spurious correlations and lacks the granularity required for practical application. In this work, we introduce FiNE-Patents (Fine-grained Novelty Examination of Patents), a novel dataset comprising 3,658 first patent claims annotated with fine-grained, feature-level prior art references extracted from European Search Opinion (ESOP) documents. We propose shifting the evaluation paradigm from simple binary classification to a joint retrieval and abstract reasoning task at the feature level, requiring models to identify specific passages from a prior art document that disclose individual claim features, and to identify which features of a claim make it novel. We implement and evaluate LLM-based workflows that decompose claims into features, analyze each feature against prior art, and finally derive a claim-level novelty prediction. Our experiments demonstrate that these workflows outperform embedding-based baselines on passage retrieval and novel feature identification. Furthermore, we show that unlike trained classifiers, LLMs are robust against spurious correlations present in the claim-level novelty classification task. We release the dataset and code to foster further research into transparent and granular patent analysis.

</details>


### [230] [Automatic Reflection Level Classification in Hungarian Student Essays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02402)
*Zsolt Csibi,Mónika Sándor,Mónika Serfőző,Kinga Gyöngy,Kristian Fenech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次对匈牙利语学生反思性写作进行自动分类研究，使用1,954篇专家标注的四级反思数据集，比较了传统机器学习模型（TF-IDF/语义嵌入）与匈牙利语特定Transformer模型，并系统处理了类别不平衡问题。传统模型综合得分达71%，Transformer模型达68%但少数类泛化更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反思性思维是教育中的关键能力，但反思性写作评估对教育专家而言耗时且主观。虽然已有多种语言的自动反思分析研究，但匈牙利语尚未得到广泛探索。

Method: 使用1,954篇跨学年收集的匈牙利语反思性论文（四级反思量表标注），采用两种方法：(1) 基于TF-IDF和语义嵌入特征的经典机器学习模型；(2) 匈牙利语特定Transformer模型进行文档级分类。通过类别加权、过采样、数据增强和替代损失函数系统处理类别不平衡问题，并进行消融实验。

Result: 经典机器学习模型在准确率、F1分数和ROC AUC上的平均综合得分达71%，Transformer模型达68%。Transformer模型在少数反思类别上泛化能力更强。

Conclusion: 经典方法在低资源场景下仍有重要价值，Transformer模型在不平衡分类中鲁棒性更优。提出的数据集和实验发现为匈牙利语及其他形态丰富语言的自动反思分析研究提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Reflective thinking is a key competency in education, but assessing reflective writing remains a time-consuming and subjective task for education experts. While automated reflective analysis has been explored in several languages, Hungarian language was not researched extensively. In this paper, we present the first comprehensive study on automatic reflection level classification in Hungarian student essays. We used a large, expert-annotated Hungarian dataset consisting of 1,954 reflective essays collected over multiple academic years and labeled on a four-level reflection scale. We investigate two approaches: (1) classical machine learning models using TF-IDF and semantic embedding features, and (2) Hungarian-specific transformer models fine-tuned for document-level reflection classification. To address the strong class imbalance in the dataset, we systematically examine class weighting, oversampling, data augmentation, and alternative loss functions. An extensive ablation study is conducted to analyze the contribution of each modeling and balancing strategy. Our results show that shallow machine learning models with appropriate feature engineering achieve strong overall performance, reaching up to 71% overall score averaged over accuracy, F1-score, and ROC AUC metrics, while transformer-based models achieve slightly lower overall score (68%) averaged over the same metrics, but demonstrate better generalization on minority reflection classes. These findings highlight the continued relevance of classical methods for low-resource settings and the robustness of transformer models for imbalanced classification. The proposed dataset and experimental insights provide a solid foundation for future research on automated reflective analysis in Hungarian and other morphologically rich languages.

</details>


### [231] [HalluScan: A Systematic Benchmark for Detecting and Mitigating Hallucinations in Instruction-Following LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02443)
*Ahmed Cherif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了HalluScan基准框架，用于系统评估大语言模型的幻觉检测与缓解方法。核心贡献包括：HalluScore复合指标（与人类专家判断的皮尔逊相关系数r=0.41）、自适应检测路由ADR（实现2.0倍成本降低，AUROC仅下降0.1%）、以及系统性的错误级联分解。实验表明NLI验证方法取得最高AUROC（0.88），RAV方法次之（0.66）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在多种NLP任务中表现优异，但仍存在生成事实错误、偏离上下文或与用户指令不一致的幻觉问题，亟需系统的评估框架来衡量各种检测与缓解方法的有效性。

Method: 构建HalluScan基准框架，涵盖6种检测方法、4个开源模型家族、3个不同领域的72种配置组合。提出HalluScore复合评估指标、自适应检测路由ADR算法，并进行错误级联分解分析。

Result: HalluScore与人类专家判断的皮尔逊相关系数为0.41；ADR实现2.0倍成本降低，AUROC仅下降0.1%；NLI验证方法取得最高AUROC（0.88），RAV方法次之（0.66）。不同领域的幻觉错误类型存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 系统性评估表明不同检测方法在不同领域表现差异显著，NLI验证方法在幻觉检测中表现最优，ADR算法可在几乎不损失性能的情况下大幅降低推理成本。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across diverse natural language processing tasks, yet they remain susceptible to hallucinations -- generating content that is factually incorrect, unfaithful to provided context, or misaligned with user instructions. We present HalluScan, a comprehensive benchmark framework that systematically evaluates hallucination detection and mitigation across 72 configurations spanning 6 detection methods, 4 open-weight model families, and 3 diverse domains. We introduce three key contributions: (1) HalluScore, a novel composite metric that achieves a Pearson correlation of r = 0.41 with human expert judgments; (2) Adaptive Detection Routing (ADR), an intelligent routing algorithm achieving 2.0x cost reduction with only 0.1% AUROC degradation; and (3) systematic error cascade decomposition revealing substantial variation in hallucination error types across domains. Our experiments reveal that NLI Verification achieves the highest overall AUROC of 0.88, while RAV achieves the second-highest AUROC of 0.66.

</details>


### [232] [PC-MNet: Dual-Level Congruity Modeling for Multimodal Sarcasm Detection via Polarity-Modulated Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02447)
*Maoheng Li,Ling Zhou,Xiaohua Huang,Rubing Huang,Wenming Zheng,Guoying Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种标量一致性路由机制与先验引导上下文图相结合的方法，通过不一致感知对比学习的两阶段非对称优化，实现多模态讽刺检测，在MUStARD基准上取得3.14%的Macro-F1提升，超越了现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作依赖基于相似性的注意力机制和统一晚期融合策略，但功能纠缠限制了传统晚期融合的表现，难以精准识别文本与非语言线索之间的语用不一致性。

Method: 引入标量一致性路由机制（scalar congruity routing mechanism）和先验引导上下文图（prior-guided contextual graph），通过不一致感知对比学习驱动两阶段非对称优化，构建广义不一致流形，选择性融合最具判别力的多粒度证据。

Result: 在MUStARD基准及其去虚假相关的平衡数据集上，相比最强多模态基线，Macro-F1提升了3.14%，达到新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: 通过架构上隔离原子冲突、组合冲突和上下文冲突，本工作为建模人类沟通中细微的语用不一致性提供了鲁棒且解耦的范式。

Abstract: Multimodal sarcasm detection, which aims to precisely identify pragmatic incongruities between literal text and nonverbal cues, has gained substantial attention in multimodal understanding. Recent advancements have predominantly relied on naïve similarity-based attention mechanisms and uniform late fusion strategies.Furthermore, given that functional entanglement restricts traditional late fusions, we incorporate a scalar congruity routing mechanism and a prior-guided contextual graph. This mechanism anchors a generalized incongruity manifold through a two-stage asymmetric optimization driven by inconsistency-aware contrastive learning, selectively fusing only the most discriminative multi-granularity evidence. Extensive experiments on the \texttt{MUStARD} benchmark and its spurious-correlation-mitigated balanced datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves new state-of-the-art performance, surpassing the strongest multimodal baseline by a substantial 3.14\% improvement in Macro-F1. By architecturally isolating atomic, composition, and contextual conflicts. This work provides a robust, decoupled paradigm for modeling subtle pragmatic incongruities in human communication.

</details>


### [233] [Leveraging Argument Structure to Predict Content Hatefulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02457)
*Nicolas Benjamin Ocampo,Davide Ceolin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨利用论证结构分析（基于WSF-ARG+数据集）来关联信息混乱现象中的不同维度（仇恨言论、虚假信息等），通过检查论证成分的可核查性和仇恨度标注，实现对整条消息仇恨程度的识别，F1分数最高达96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息混乱（包括仇恨言论、虚假信息、误导信息等）是一个影响社会的广泛问题，现有研究多针对单一维度，缺乏将这些不同维度联系起来的方法。本文动机是利用论证结构作为桥梁，链接这些不同领域的问题。

Method: 使用WSF-ARG+数据集（白人至上主义论坛消息，已标注论证结构的前提和结论），利用论证组件的可核查性（checkworthiness）和仇恨度（hatefulness）标注，分析整条消息的仇恨程度。

Result: 实验取得了最高96%的F1分数，表明该方向具有良好前景。

Conclusion: 论证结构分析有望扩展到仇恨内容识别和信息混乱对抗的更多应用场景中。

Abstract: Information disorder is a challenging phenomenon that affects society at large. This phenomenon entails the diffusion of misleading, misinforming, and hateful content online. In different contexts, one aspect of the problem may prevail, but overall, this is a broad problem that requires comprehensive solutions. While each dimension of the problem (hate speech, disinformation, misinformation, etc.) requires in-depth analysis, in this paper, we look into the possibility of argument structure to provide relevant information to link these different areas of the problem. In particular, we focus on the WSF-ARG+ dataset, which consists of white supremacy forum messages annotated in terms of argument structure (premises and conclusion). There, we leverage the checkworthiness and hatefulness annotations of the argument components to obtain insights into the hatefulness of the whole message. Our results show promising insights (up to 96% F1), indicating the possibility of extending this direction in the future to tackle hateful content identification and information disorder countering.

</details>


### [234] [ATLAS: Article Tracking, Linking, and Analysis of Swedish Encyclopedias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02466)
*Albin Andersson,Salam Jonasson,Fredrik Wastring,Pierre Nugues*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一套自动化流水线，用于从数字化但缺乏结构的历史百科全书文本中恢复条目结构、分类实体、跨版本匹配条目并链接到Wikidata，在瑞典语百科全书《Nordisk familjebok》四个主要版本上取得了良好的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旧版百科全书的数字化通常仅停留在光学字符识别层面，未能利用其内部结构信息，且多版本间的知识演化难以追踪。为了改善对历史结构化知识的访问并理解知识传播过程，需要自动恢复文本结构。

Method: 构建了一条流水线，包含四个步骤：提取词头并识别条目、对实体进行分类、跨版本匹配条目、将条目链接到Wikidata。将该方法应用于瑞典百科全书《Nordisk familjebok》1876年至1951年间的四个主要版本。

Result: 词头提取F1分数为97.8%，词头分类F1分数为93.4%。跨版本匹配精确率达到93%；Wikidata链接精确率为85%，召回率为16.5%。

Conclusion: 自动化方法处理数字化历史知识是可行的，有助于促进通用知识的保存和理解知识传播。

Abstract: The digitization of old encyclopedias represents an important step to improve access to historically structured knowledge. Often, however, this process does not go beyond an optical character recognition, leaving all the underlying structure unexploited. In addition, many encyclopedias had multiple editions reflecting the evolution of knowledge. The lack of structure in the raw text makes it difficult to track changes across these editions. In this work, we built a pipeline to restore the text structure, where we extract the headwords and identify entries; categorize the entities; match entries across editions; and link entries to a Wikidata item. We applied this pipeline to the four major editions of \textit{Nordisk familjebok}, an authoritative Swedish encyclopedia published between 1876 and 1951. We could extract the headwords with an F1 score of 97.8\% and we obtained an F1 score of 93.4\% on the headword classification. On a small-scale evaluation, we reached a 93\% precision on the cross-edition matching, 85\% precision and 16.5\% recall on the Wikidata linking. This shows that an automated approach to digitized historical knowledge is possible. This should facilitate the preservation of general knowledge and the understanding of knowledge transmission. The datasets and programs are available online.

</details>


### [235] [Accurate Legal Reasoning at Scale: Neuro-Symbolic Offloading and Structural Auditability for Robust Legal Adjudication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02472)
*Stanisław Sójka,Witold Kowalczyk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号方法——摊销智能（Amortized Intelligence），通过将法律文本一次性翻译为确定性自主合约语言（DACL）图中间表示，随后基于确定性图执行进行裁决，相比运行时大推理模型（LRM）基线（包括GPT-5.2和Gemini 3 Pro），实现了接近完美的推理一致性，避免了概率模型的"推理悬崖"问题，计算成本降低超90%，并满足严格的可审计性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律文本中的计算性法律条款需要复杂逻辑理解，而现有前沿大推理模型虽能描述这些条款，但在构建生产级系统时受限于推理错误和高昂的推理成本。

Method: 提出摊销智能（Amortized Intelligence）这一神经符号方法：使用LLM一次性将法律文本翻译为确定性自主合约语言（DACL）——一种带类型的图中间表示；裁决阶段则依赖确定性图执行，并生成可视化的可审计轨迹。

Result: 相比运行时LRM基线（GPT-5.2和Gemini 3 Pro），DACL智能体实现了接近完美的推理一致性，消除了概率模型的"推理悬崖"问题；在高吞吐量工作流中计算成本降低超90%。

Conclusion: 该神经符号方法在保证严格可审计性的前提下，有效解决了LRM在法律裁决场景中的推理不一致和高成本问题，为法律文本自动化处理提供了可行的生产级解决方案。

Abstract: Legal texts often contain computational legal clauses--provisions whose understanding requires complex logic. While frontier Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) can describe such clauses, building production-ready systems is limited by reasoning errors and the high cost of inference. We propose Amortized Intelligence, a neuro-symbolic approach where we use an LLM once to translate a legal text into Deterministic Autonomous Contract Language (DACL): a typed graph intermediate representation. Adjudication then relies on deterministic graph executions with a visually auditable trace. In comparison against runtime LRM baselines (including GPT-5.2 and Gemini 3 Pro), our DACL-based Agent achieves near-perfect consistency and mitigates the "reasoning cliff" observed in probabilistic models. The system reduces compute costs by over 90% in high-volume workflows while satisfying the strict auditability requirements of legal adjudication.

</details>


### [236] [A multilingual hallucination benchmark: MultiWikiQHalluA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02504)
*Freja Thoresen,Dan Saattrup Smart*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对多语言（306种语言）中的 faithfulness 幻觉（忠实性幻觉）问题，利用 MultiWikiQA 数据集和 LettuceDetect 框架构建了30种欧洲语言的词级幻觉分类器，并评估了5种不同规模模型在英语、丹麦语、德语和冰岛语上的幻觉率。结果显示，小模型（Qwen3-0.6B）幻觉率高达60%，而大模型表现更好；低资源语言（尤其是冰岛语）的幻觉率普遍更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉评估研究主要集中在英语上，缺乏对低资源语言的研究，因此需要系统性地评估多语言场景下的忠实性幻觉。

Method: 利用多语言 MultiWikiQA 数据集，通过 LettuceDetect 框架为306种语言合成幻觉数据集，进而训练30种欧洲语言的词级幻觉分类器，并评估5种模型（Qwen3-0.6B/14B、Gemma-3-12B-IT、cogito-v1-preview-qwen-32B/llama-70B）在4种语言上的幻觉率。

Result: Qwen3-0.6B 幻觉率最高（冰岛语上超60%），大模型表现更好；cogito-v1-preview 系列表现最优；低资源语言幻觉率显著高于高资源语言。

Conclusion: 低资源语言的忠实性幻觉问题更为突出，模型规模与语言资源丰富度均与幻觉率呈负相关，需要更多针对低资源语言的研究和优化。

Abstract: Most hallucination evaluations focus on English, leaving it unclear whether findings transfer to lower-resource languages. We investigate faithfulness hallucinations, defined as model-generated content that is fluent and plausible but diverges from the provided input or is internally inconsistent. Leveraging the multilingual MultiWikiQA dataset, we utilize the LettuceDetect framework to create synthetic hallucination datasets for 306 languages, from which we train token-level hallucination classifiers for 30 European languages. In this work, we present evaluations of model hallucinations on a selection of languages: English, Danish, German, and Icelandic. Using these classifiers, we evaluate the hallucination rates for Qwen3-0.6B, Qwen3-14B, Gemma-3-12B-IT, cogito-v1-preview-qwen-32B, and cogito-v1-preview-llama-70B. Our classifiers reveal notably higher hallucination rates for Qwen3-0.6B (up to 60\% of answers containing at least one hallucination, peaking in Icelandic) and generally lower rates for larger models, with cogito-v1-preview-qwen-32B and cogito-v1-preview-llama-70B performing best on most languages. Hallucination rates are consistently higher for lower-resource languages, particularly Icelandic.

</details>


### [237] [Revisiting Semantic Role Labeling: Efficient Structured Inference with Dependency-Informed Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02505)
*Sangpil Youm,Leah Jones,Bonnie J. Dorr*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个现代化的基于编码器的结构化语义角色标注（SRL）框架，在保持显式谓词-参数结构的同时，实现了10倍推理速度提升，并展示了其在多语言SRL投影中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SRL系统依赖已进入维护模式的AllenNLP，难以兼容现代编码器架构；同时，大型语言模型缺乏显式结构约束和可解释性机制，亟需一个兼顾结构显式性和推理效率的现代化框架。

Method: 构建基于编码器（BERT/RoBERTa/DeBERTa）的现代化SRL框架，采用依赖关系感知的诊断方法分析跨度级不一致性，并在表示层面分析LLM在依赖结构信号下的行为。

Result: 基于BERT-base的模型在保持可比预测性能的同时实现10倍推理加速；RoBERTa和DeBERTa在同框架下进一步提升F1；依赖线索主要提升结构稳定性。

Conclusion: 现代化的结构化SRL框架能够有效平衡预测性能与推理效率，其显式谓词-参数结构可支持多语言SRL投影等下游应用。

Abstract: Semantic Role Labeling (SRL) provides an explicit representation of predicate-argument structure, capturing linguistically grounded relations such as who did what to whom. While recent NLP progress has been dominated by large language models (LLMs), these systems often rely on implicit semantic representations, often lacking explicit structural constraints and systematic explanatory mechanisms. Traditionally, SRL systems have often relied on AllenNLP; however, the framework entered maintenance mode in December 2022, limiting compatibility with evolving encoder architectures and modern inference requirements. We revisit structured SRL modeling, introducing a modernized encoder-based framework that preserves explicit predicate-argument structure while enabling inference 10 times faster. Using BERT-base, the model attains comparable predictive performance, and RoBERTa and DeBERTa further improve F1 performance within the same framework. We adopt a dependency-informed diagnostic methodology to characterize span-level inconsistencies and conduct a representation-level analysis of LLM behavior under dependency-informed structural signals. Results indicate that dependency cues primarily improve structural stability. Finally, we illustrate how the framework's explicit predicate-argument structure can support multilingual SRL projection as a downstream application.

</details>


### [238] [Benchmarking Retrieval Strategies for Biomedical Retrieval-Augmented Generation: A Controlled Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02520)
*Devi Prasad Bal,Subhashree Puhan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了五种检索策略（稠密向量检索、混合BM25+稠密检索、交叉编码器重排序、多查询扩展、最大边际相关性）在生物医学RAG管道中的效果，使用固定生成模型和统一评估框架。交叉编码器重排序取得了最佳综合分数（0.827），所有RAG策略在答案相关性上均显著优于无检索基线，证实了检索在生物医学QA中的实际价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险的生物医学领域，RAG的检索策略选择至关重要，但此前缺乏受控的、多指标的系统比较研究。作者旨在填补这一空白，为生物医学QA中的检索策略选择提供实证依据。

Method: 在固定GPT-4o-mini生成模型、ChromaDB向量存储和text-embedding-3-small嵌入的统一框架下，比较五种检索策略。使用250对BioASQ问答数据，通过DeepEval的四个指标（上下文精确度、上下文召回率、忠实度、答案相关性）进行评估，含95%置信区间和无检索消融基线。

Result: 交叉编码器重排序综合得分最高（0.827），上下文精确度达0.852；多查询扩展上下文精确度最弱（0.671）；稠密基线综合得分0.822，与最优策略仅差0.005；所有RAG条件答案相关性（0.658-0.701）远超无检索基线（0.287）。

Conclusion: 交叉编码器重排序在生物医学RAG中表现最优；稠密检索作为基线竞争力强；多查询扩展的朴素查询多样化会引入检索噪声；检索对生物医学QA具有不可替代的实际价值。代码与超参数已公开。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) offers a well-established path to grounding large language model (LLM) outputs in external knowledge, yet the question of which retrieval strategy works best in a high-stakes domain such as biomedicine has not received the controlled, multi-metric treatment it deserves. This paper presents a systematic empirical comparison of five retrieval strategies -- Dense Vector Search, Hybrid BM25 + Dense retrieval, Cross-Encoder Reranking, Multi-Query Expansion, and Maximal Marginal Relevance (MMR) -- within a biomedical question-answering RAG pipeline. All strategies share a fixed generation model (GPT-4o-mini), a common vector store (ChromaDB), and OpenAI's text-embedding-3-small embeddings, ensuring that observed differences are attributable to retrieval alone. Evaluation is conducted on 250 question-answer pairs drawn from a preprocessed subset of the BioASQ benchmark (rag-mini-bioasq) using four DeepEval metrics: contextual precision, contextual recall, faithfulness, and answer relevancy, each reported with 95% confidence intervals. A no-context ablation is included as a lower bound. Cross-Encoder Reranking achieves the best composite score (0.827) and highest contextual precision (0.852), confirming that query-document interaction yields measurable retrieval gains. Multi-Query Expansion, despite its recall-oriented design, produces the weakest contextual precision (0.671), suggesting naive query diversification introduces retrieval noise. MMR sacrifices answer relevancy for diversity, while the Dense baseline (composite 0.822) falls within 0.005 points of the top strategy. All RAG conditions dramatically outperform the no-context ablation on answer relevancy (0.658-0.701 vs. 0.287), confirming the practical value of retrieval. The full pipeline, hyperparameters, and evaluation code are publicly available.

</details>


### [239] [SemEval-2026 Task 7: Everyday Knowledge Across Diverse Languages and Cultures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02601)
*Nedjma Ousidhoum,Junho Myung,Carla Perez-Almendros,Jiho Jin,Amr Keleg,Meriem Beloucif,Yi Zhou,Rodrigo Agerri,Vladimir Araujo,Naomi Baes,James Barry,Joanne Boisson,Nancy F. Chen,Christine de Kock,Aleksandra Edwards,Joseba Fernandez de Landa,Mohamed Fazli Imam,Huda Hakami,Shu-Kai Hsieh,Joseph Marvin Imperial,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Zhengyuan Liu,Chenyang Lyu,Younes Samih,Johan Sjons,Bryan Tan,Asahi Ushio,Weihua Zheng,Alice Oh,Jose Camacho-Collados*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个关于评估LLM和NLP系统在多语言和跨文化环境中的适应性的共享任务。任务基于扩展的BLEnD基准，涵盖30多种语言-文化对，主要为低资源语言。设有两道赛道：简答题和多项选择题。吸引超过140名注册参与者，收到62个最终提交和19篇系统描述论文。报告了最佳系统的结果和常用方法，讨论了低资源语言和代表性不足文化中的评估、失调和方法学挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在评估LLM和NLP系统在多种语言和文化（尤其是低资源语言和代表性不足文化）中的适应性，填补当前NLP评估在多语言跨文化泛化能力上的空白。

Method: 采用扩展的BLEnD基准（30+语言-文化对），设置SAQ和MCQ两个评估赛道，严格禁止参与者使用数据进行训练或微调，允许提交任意NLP系统和多样化建模策略。

Result: 140+注册参与者，62个最终提交，19篇系统描述论文；报告了最佳系统的表现以及最常用的方法路径。

Conclusion: 共享了关于低资源语言和代表性不足文化中评估、失调和方法学视角的开放性问题和挑战的洞察。

Abstract: We present our shared task on evaluating the adaptability of LLMs and NLP systems across multiple languages and cultures. The task data consist of an extended version of our manually constructed BLEnD benchmark (Myung et al. 2024), covering more than 30 language-culture pairs, predominantly representing low-resource languages spoken across multiple continents. As the task is designed strictly for evaluation, participants were not permitted to use the data for training, fine-tuning, few-shot learning, or any other form of model modification. Our task includes two tracks: (a) Short-Answer Questions (SAQ) and (b) Multiple-Choice Questions (MCQ). Participants were required to predict labels and were allowed to submit any NLP system and adopt diverse modelling strategies, provided that the benchmark was used solely for evaluation. The task attracted more than 140 registered participants, and we received final submissions from 62 teams, along with 19 system description papers. We report the results and present an analysis of the best-performing systems and the most commonly adopted approaches. Furthermore, we discuss shared insights into open questions and challenges related to evaluation, misalignment, and methodological perspectives on model behaviour in low-resource languages and for under-represented cultures.

</details>


### [240] [Dependency Parsing Across the Resource Spectrum: Evaluating Architectures on High and Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02608)
*Kevin Guan,Happy Buzaaba,Christiane Fellbaum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在低资源场景下，Biaffine LSTM解析器一致优于Transformer模型，而Transformer的优势仅在训练数据充足时恢复；形态复杂度是Transformer相对劣势的次要预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Transformer模型在高资源语言的依存句法分析中已达最优水平，但它们在低资源环境下的表现优势是否依然成立尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在10种类型多样的语言（重点为低资源非洲语言）上，评估四种解析器：Biaffine LSTM、Stack-Pointer Network、AfroXLMR-large和RemBERT。控制语料库规模后，使用MATTR指标衡量形态复杂度作为预测变量。

Result: 低资源场景下Biaffine LSTM一致优于Transformer模型；Transformer的优势随训练数据增加而恢复，交叉点落在低资源树库典型规模范围内；形态复杂度（MATTR）是Transformer相对劣势的显著次要预测因子。

Conclusion: 在低资源环境下，Biaffine LSTM更适合句法工具开发，直到拥有足够的标注数据来发挥预训练Transformer的表示能力。

Abstract: Transformer-based models achieve state-of-the-art dependency parsing for high-resource languages, yet their advantage over simpler architectures in low-resource settings remains poorly understood. We evaluate four parsers -- the Biaffine LSTM, Stack-Pointer Network, AfroXLMR-large, and RemBERT -- across ten typologically diverse languages, with a focus on low-resource African languages. We find that the Biaffine LSTM consistently outperforms transformer models in low-resource regimes, with transformers recovering their advantage as training data increases. The crossover falls within a resource range typical of treebanks for under-resourced languages. Morphological complexity (measured via MATTR) emerges as a significant secondary predictor of transformers' relative disadvantage after controlling for corpus size. These results indicate that the Biaffine LSTM may be better suited for syntactic tool development in low-resource regimes until sufficient annotated data is available to leverage the representational capacity of pre-trained transformers.

</details>


### [241] [Synthetic Users, Real Differences: an Evaluation Framework for User Simulation in Multi-Turn Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02624)
*Yu Lu Liu,Hyokun Yun,Tanya Roosta,Ziang Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RealSim评估框架，从8个维度（交互沟通功能、用户状态、用户消息表面形式）对真实与模拟对话进行分布级比较，发现模拟用户难以捕捉真实用户引入的沟通摩擦，可能导致评估过于乐观，且不同领域表现存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户模拟真实性的评估方法仅提供粗粒度质量信号且停留在单轮对话层面，缺乏系统性、多维度的评估框架，难以支持更严格的模拟真实性评估。

Method: 提出RealSim评估框架，从8个维度（涵盖交互沟通功能、用户状态、用户消息表面形式）进行真实与模拟对话的分布级比较；构建包含1K多轮任务导向真实对话数据集，覆盖16个聊天机器人应用领域。

Result: 模拟用户难以捕捉真实用户引入的沟通摩擦，可能导致基于模拟的评估结果过于乐观；不同领域间模拟性能存在显著差异，表明可能需要领域特定的用户模拟器。

Conclusion: RealSim框架能更严格地评估模拟真实性，揭示了现有模拟在捕捉真实用户行为方面的不足，为改进用户模拟器提供了方向。

Abstract: There is growing interest in exploring user simulation as an alternative to gathering and scoring real user-chatbot interactions for AI chatbot evaluation. For this purpose, it is important to ensure the realism of the simulation, i.e., the extent to which simulated dialogues reflect real dialogues users have with chatbots. Most existing methods evaluating simulation realism produce coarse quality signal and remain solely at the level of individual dialogues. To support more rigorous evaluation in this area, we propose realsim, an evaluation framework that enables practitioners to take a distributional view of real vs. simulated dialogues along 8 dimensions, covering attributes related to the communicative functions of the interaction, user states, and the surface form of user messages. We then instantiate the framework with a curated dataset of 1K multi-turn task-focused real user-chatbot dialogues that cover 16 domains of chatbot applications. Overall, we find that simulated users tend to struggle at capturing communication frictions that real users introduce to interactions, which could make evaluations based on such simulations overly optimistic. We also observe variability in performance across different domains, which may indicate a need for domain-specific user simulators.

</details>


### [242] [ContextualJailbreak: Evolutionary Red-Teaming via Simulated Conversational Priming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02647)
*Mario Rodríguez Béjar,Francisco J. Cortés-Delgado,S. Braghin,Jose L. Hernández-Ramos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ContextualJailbreak，一种基于进化搜索的黑盒红队策略，通过五项语义变异算子在模拟多轮对话中生成越狱攻击，在HarmBench基准上达到最高100%的攻击成功率，并发现不同供应商模型之间的对齐鲁棒性存在显著不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化红队方法主要局限于单轮场景，缺乏对多轮对话中上下文引导（contextual priming）这一强大攻击面的探索；针对有效引导对话的变异算子设计空间尚未被充分研究。

Method: 采用黑盒进化搜索策略，在模拟的多轮引导对话上执行搜索；使用两级评判器（two-level judge）生成0-5分级危害分数作为循环信号，允许部分有害响应引导搜索；定义了五种语义变异算子：角色扮演(roleplay)、场景(scenario)、扩展(expand)、故障排除(troubleshooting)和机制性(mechanistic)，其中后两种为本文新贡献。

Result: 在50个HarmBench行为上，对gpt-oss:20B、qwen3-8B、llama3.1:70B实现100% ASR，对gpt-oss:120B实现90% ASR，平均超越四种基线31-96个百分点；迁移攻击在gpt-4o-mini达90.0%、gpt-5达70.0%、gemini-3-flash达70.0%，但在claude-opus-4-7仅17.5%、claude-sonnet-4-6仅15.0%。

Conclusion: 基于进化搜索的多轮越狱攻击策略有效且高效，揭示了不同供应商（尤其是Anthropic vs OpenAI/Google）在对齐鲁棒性上存在显著的不对称性，表明需要针对多轮上下文攻击进行更鲁棒的对齐训练。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that bypass safety alignment and elicit harmful responses. A growing body of work shows that contextual priming, where earlier turns covertly bias later replies, constitutes a powerful attack surface, with hand-crafted multi-turn scaffolds consistently outperforming single-turn manipulations on capable models.
  However, automated optimization-based red-teaming has remained largely limited to the single-turn setting, iterating over static prompts and lacking the ability to reason about which forms of conversational priming induce compliance. While recent multi-turn, search-based approaches have begun to bridge this gap, the mutator design space underlying effective primed dialogues remains largely unexplored.
  We present ContextualJailbreak, a black-box red-teaming strategy that performs evolutionary search over a simulated multi-turn primed dialogue. The strategy leverages a graded 0-5 harm score from a two-level judge as an in-loop signal, enabling partially harmful responses to guide the search process rather than being discarded.
  Search is driven by five semantically defined mutation operators: roleplay, scenario, expand, troubleshooting, and mechanistic, of which the last two are novel contributions of this work.
  Across 50 representative HarmBench behaviors, ContextualJailbreak achieves an ASR of 100% on gpt-oss:20B, 100% on qwen3-8B, 100% on llama3.1:70B, and 90% on gpt-oss:120B, outperforming four single- and multi-turn baselines by 31-96 percentage points on average.
  The 40 maximally harmful attacks discovered against gpt-oss:120B transfer without adaptation to closed frontier models, achieving 90.0% on gpt-4o-mini, 70.0% on gpt-5, and 70.0% on gemini-3-flash, but only 17.5% on claude-opus-4-7 and 15.0% on claude-sonnet-4-6, revealing a pronounced provider-level asymmetry in alignment robustness.

</details>


### [243] [Fuzzy Fingerprinting Encoder Pre-trained Language Models for Emotion Recognition in Conversations: Human Assessment and Validity Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02665)
*Patrícia Pereira,Helena Moniz,Joao Paulo Carvalho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将预训练语言模型（PLMs）与模糊指纹（FFPs）相结合的可解释方法，用于对话情感识别（ERC）。FFP通过生成类别特定的原型，减少模型对中性类别的过度分类，并在保持SOTA性能的同时提供分类过程的洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PLM在ERC任务中缺乏可解释性，尤其在类别不平衡的数据集上，模型倾向于将少数情感类别误分类为多数中性类别。现有方法难以提供分类过程的见解。

Method: 1) 从PLM的潜在空间中，通过排序和模糊化每个情感类别的池化上下文相关嵌入激活值，生成类别特定的模糊指纹（FFP）原型；2) 推理时，对输入话语生成模糊指纹，并通过基于模糊集合交集的模糊相似度函数与情感原型进行匹配。

Result: FFP集成减少了中性类别的过度分类，人类评估进一步验证了FFP预测的充分性，性能达到SOTA水平。

Conclusion: 该方法弥合了深度神经推理与人类感知之间的差距，在保持SOTA性能的同时提供分类过程的可解释性。

Abstract: In Emotion Recognition in Conversations (ERC), model decisions should align with nuanced human perception and ideally provide insights on the classification process. Standard encoder pre-trained language models (PLMs) are the state-of-the-art at these tasks but offer little insight into why a certain prediction is made. This is especially problematic in imbalanced datasets, where most utterances are labeled as neutral, making these models frequently misclassify minority emotions as the majority neutral class. To tackle this issue, we introduced a novel, interpretable approach to ERC by combining PLMs with Fuzzy Fingerprints (FFPs). FFP provide class-specific prototypes that reflect the characteristic class activation patterns in the PLM's latent space. They are derived by ranking and fuzzifying the activations of the pooled conversational context-dependent embeddings across training instances for each emotion. At inference time, each input utterance is similarly fuzzy fingerprinted and matched to the emotion prototypes using a fuzzy similarity function based on the aggregation of the intersection of the fuzzy sets that define each FFP. Experimental results show that FFP integration reduces overclassification into the neutral class and human evaluation further supports the adequacy of FFP predictions. Our proposed method thus bridges the gap between deep neural inference and human perception, performing at state-of-the-art level while simultaneously offering valuable insights into the classification procedure.

</details>


### [244] [mdok-style at SemEval-2026 Task 10: Finetuning LLMs for Conspiracy Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02712)
*Dominik Macko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对SemEval-2026 Task 10的阴谋论检测任务，提出mdok-style系统。通过数据增强和自训练策略（应对训练数据不足），微调Qwen3-32B模型进行二分类文本分类。该系统在52个提交中排名第8（85百分位），证明了原本用于机器生成文本检测的方法也可有效用于阴谋论检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SemEval-2026 Task 10聚焦于阴谋论检测，旨在识别Reddit评论是否表达阴谋论信念。训练数据量较小是主要挑战，需要有效的数据利用策略。

Method: 采用数据增强和自训练策略应对训练数据不足，基于Qwen3-32B模型进行微调，将其转化为二分类文本分类任务。

Result: 在52个提交中排名第8（85百分位），表现极具竞争力。

Conclusion: 源于机器生成文本检测的方法可迁移应用于阴谋论检测任务，证明了方法的跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: SemEval-2026 Task 10 is focused on conspiracy detection. Specifically, the goal is to detect whether a Reddit comment expresses a conspiracy belief. Our submitted mdok-style system utilizes data augmentation and self-training (to cope with a rather small amount of training data) to finetune the Qwen3-32B model for a binary text-classification task. The submitted system is very competitive, ranking in the 85th percentile (8th out of 52 submissions). The results shown that our approach, which originated in machine-generated text detection, can be used for conspiracy detection as well.

</details>


### [245] [FlexSQL: Flexible Exploration and Execution Make Better Text-to-SQL Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02815)
*Quang Hieu Pham,Yang He,Ping Nie,Canwen Xu,Davood Rafiei,Yuepeng Wang,Xi Ye,Jocelyn Qiaochu Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FlexSQL 提出了一种灵活数据库交互的 Text-to-SQL 智能体设计，通过灵活的 schema 探索、多样化执行计划和两级修复机制，在 Spider2-Snow 上以较小模型（gpt-oss-120b）超越更强更大的基线模型（如 GPT-o3、DeepSeek-R1），并作为技能集成到通用编码智能体中提升超 10% 的相对性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Text-to-SQL 系统采用固定流水线：schema 元素仅一次性预检索，数据库仅在事后修复阶段被访问，这限制了从早期错误中恢复的能力。因此需要一种更灵活的设计，允许在推理的任何阶段与数据库交互。

Method: FlexSQL 包含三个核心组件：1) 灵活探索（flexible exploration）：允许在推理任意阶段探索 schema 结构、检查数据值、运行验证查询；2) 多样化执行计划（diverse execution plans）：生成覆盖多种查询解释的不同执行计划，并根据任务用 SQL 或 Python 实现；3) 两级修复机制（two-tiered repair）：支持从代码级错误回溯到计划级修订。

Result: 在 Spider2-Snow 基准上，使用 gpt-oss-120b 模型，FlexSQL 达到 65.4% 的分数，超越了使用更强更大模型（如 GPT-o3 和 DeepSeek-R1）的强开源基线。作为技能集成到 Claude Code 通用编码智能体中，带来超过 10% 的相对性能提升。

Conclusion: 灵活探索和灵活执行共同构成了该方法有效性的基础，突出"灵活性"作为 Text-to-SQL 系统设计的关键原则。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL over large analytical databases requires navigating complex schemas, resolving ambiguous queries, and grounding decisions in actual data. Most current systems follow a fixed pipeline where schema elements are retrieved once upfront and the database is only revisited for post-hoc repair, limiting recovery from early mistakes. We present FlexSQL, a text-to-SQL agent whose core design principle is flexible database interaction: the agent can explore schema structure, inspect data values, and run verification queries at any point during reasoning. FlexSQL generates diverse execution plans to cover multiple query interpretations, implements each plan in either SQL or Python depending on the task, and uses a two-tiered repair mechanism that can backtrack from code-level errors to plan-level revisions. On Spider2-Snow, using gpt-oss-120b, FlexSQL achieves a 65.4\% score, outperforming strong open-source baselines that use stronger, larger models such as gpt-o3 and DeepSeek-R1. When integrated into a general-purpose coding agent (as skills in Claude Code), our approach yields over 10\% relative improvement on Spider2-Snow. Further analysis shows that flexible exploration and flexible execution jointly contribute to the effectiveness of our approach, highlighting flexibility as a key design principle. Our code is available at: https://github.com/StringNLPLAB/FlexSQL

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [246] [How Historians Use Visualization: A Corpus-Backed Taxonomy and Analysis for Cross-Disciplinary Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01456)
*Xinyue Chen,Yu Zhang,Weili Zheng,Chiteng Ma,Xiaoru Yuan*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析4,142篇历史学和数字人文学术论文中的14,021张图像，结合可视化分类体系和半自动标注流程，识别出历史研究中可视化的五种角色（原始史料、证据综合、沟通、确认、探索），并揭示了阻碍可视化采纳的认识论和实践障碍（不确定性、来源追溯、论证负担、出版限制）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史研究中的可视化正从孤立尝试转向系统实践，但关于历史学家实际如何使用可视化的数据驱动证据仍然匮乏。

Method: 采用语料库驱动的混合方法研究：从4,142篇研究论文中构建14,021张图像的语料库；使用层次化、领域信息化的分类体系对4,831个可视化实例进行分类；结合11位历史学家的访谈以及HiFigAtlas系统作为边界对象支持语料库的联合检查。

Result: 识别出可视化在历史研究中的五种不同角色：原始史料、证据综合、沟通、确认和探索；发现历史学家使用图表追求多样化目标，但存在持续的认识论和实践障碍（不确定性、来源追溯、论证负担、出版限制）阻碍可视化采纳。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了历史学术中可视化使用的扎实描述，指出了更好地支持领域特定需求的机会。

Abstract: Visualization in historical research is shifting from isolated attempts to systematic practices. However, data-driven evidence about how historians actually use visualization remains scarce. We present a corpus-driven, mixed-methods study that combines analysis of images from 4,142 research articles across history and digital humanities journals with a collaboratively developed visualization taxonomy and a semi-automatic labeling pipeline. We construct a corpus of 14,021 images, classify 4,831 visualization instances using a hierarchical, domain-informed taxonomy, and analyze patterns of visualization adoption across venues, history subfields, and time. To interpret these patterns, we conduct interviews with 11 historians and use HiFigAtlas system as a boundary object to support joint inspection of the corpus. We identify distinct roles for visualizations in historical research: primary-source, evidence-synthesis, communicative, confirmative, and exploratory. We further find that while historians pursue diverse goals with figures, persistent epistemological and practical barriers, such as uncertainty, provenance, justification burden, and publication constraints, impede the adoption of visualization. This work contributes a grounded account of visualization use in historical scholarship and points to opportunities to better support domain-specific needs.

</details>


### [247] [The Antipodal Method: Fast, Accurate, and Robust 3D Generalized Winding Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01536)
*Cedric Martens,Philip Trettner,Mikhail Bessmeltsev*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的广义缠绕数计算方法，通过将缠绕数分解为射线-曲面交点的符号计数和曲面在单位球面上投影的边界积分，实现了对网格和参数曲面的快速、精确计算。在CPU上比最快精确方法快22倍，GPU上可达10^9次查询/秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有广义缠绕数计算方法在精度和计算效率之间存在权衡，限制了其在交互式和大规模几何处理任务中的应用。

Method: 将缠绕数分解为两个直观的几何量之和：射线-曲面交点的符号计数，以及曲面在单位球面上投影的边界积分。这种分解避免了昂贵的面积分和球面排列，实现了高效的离散化计算。

Result: 对于网格：CPU上平均比最快精确方法快22倍，比最快近似方法快3倍，同时保持全精度。GPU上中等复杂度网格可达10^9次查询/秒，或4000张缠绕数切片以120 FPS渲染（比朴素GPU方法快13倍）。对于参数曲面：平均比现有方法快5.6倍，精度相同。

Conclusion: 该方法在精度、速度和鲁棒性方面均优于现有方法，能自然处理复杂拓扑和非流形输入，并已开源。

Abstract: Generalized winding numbers provide a robust measure of point insidedness for 3D surfaces - whether open, self-intersecting, or non-manifold - and are central to numerous geometry processing tasks. However, existing methods trade off between accuracy and computational efficiency, limiting their use in interactive and large-scale applications.
  We introduce a new formulation and algorithm for computing generalized winding numbers that is both fast and accurate to arbitrary precision, applicable to meshes and parametric surfaces. Our approach expresses the winding number as the sum of two intuitive geometric quantities: the signed number of ray-surface intersections and a boundary integral over the surface's projection onto the unit sphere. This insight leads to an efficient discretization that avoids expensive surface integrals and spherical arrangements.
  For meshes, our method achieves average speedups of $22\times$ on a CPU compared to the fastest precise methods and $3\times$ compared to the fastest approximation method, while maintaining full precision. On a GPU, for moderately complex meshes we reach a throughput of $10^9$ queries per second, or $4K$ generalized winding number slices at 120 FPS ($13\times$ faster than a naive GPU method). For parametric surfaces, our method is on average $5.6\times$ faster than the state-of-the-art method, with the same precision. Our method naturally handles complex topologies and non-manifold inputs. We extensively validate its accuracy, robustness, and time performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/MartensCedric/antipodal.

</details>


### [248] [Greed for the Spheres: A Signed Distance Interpolation Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01919)
*Letao Chen,Sanju Mupparaju,Christopher Batty,Silvia Sellán,Oded Stein*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从离散样本插值有符号距离函数(SDF)的方法，通过将SDF的理论性质表示为硬几何约束，并设计高效的贪心算法（辅以并行化GPU预处理），确保插值数据完全一致且对应几何上可实现的表面。在全局SDF细化、网格重建和伪SDF修复三个应用中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SDF插值方法无法保证插值后的数据与输入数据之间以及数据内部保持一致性，导致结果不对应于几何上可实现的表面，缺乏理论保证。

Method: 将SDF的理论性质（如一致性约束）转化为硬几何约束条件，构建贪心算法进行一致的SDF插值，并利用GPU并行预处理加速。

Result: 在全局SDF细化、网格重建和伪SDF修复三个任务上验证了方法有效性，生成的SDF保证与输入一致，优于无此保证的现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文方法能够生成与输入完全一致的SDF数据，确保对应几何可实现的表面，在多个应用中表现优越。

Abstract: We propose a method to interpolate Signed Distance Function (SDF) data from a discrete set of samples. Unlike prior work, our approach ensures that the new SDF data values are fully consistent with the input and each other, such that the augmented data still corresponds to a geometrically realizable surface. We express the theoretical properties of SDFs as hard geometric constraints, and construct an efficient greedy algorithm for consistent SDF interpolation that is made even faster with powerful parallelized GPU preprocessing. We exemplify the usefulness of our method by evaluating it on three practical applications: global SDF refinement, in which the SDF data is upsampled without knowledge of the ground truth; mesh reconstruction, where our method can reconstruct highly detailed surfaces using global information from coarse input SDFs; and repair of pseudo-SDFs, which result from many pipelines such as CSG Boolean operations and must be turned into valid SDFs for downstream processing tasks. Our refined SDFs are guaranteed to be consistent with the input, where previous methods have no such guarantee.

</details>


### [249] [Generative Modeling with Orbit-Space Particle Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02222)
*Sinan Wang,Jinjin He,Shenyifan Lu,Ruicheng Wang,Greg Turk,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: OGPP是一个基于轨道空间几何概率路径的粒子原生流匹配框架，通过轨道空间规范化、粒子索引嵌入和几何概率路径三个关键组件，在最少表面基准、ShapeNet和单形状编码任务上实现了显著性能提升，包括将度量误差降低两个数量级、5倍步数达到SOTA、26倍更少参数达到DiT-3D性能等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个核心洞见：(i) 粒子具有排列对称性，匿名索引会膨胀每索引目标方差并产生弯曲、难学的流；(ii) 粒子存在于物理空间，因此流终端速度具有物理意义并可编码几何属性（如表面法线）。

Method: 三个关键组件：(1) 概率路径终端端点的轨道空间规范化；(2) 用于角色特化的粒子索引嵌入；(3) 具有弧长感知终端速度的几何概率路径，将法线作为流的副产品生成。

Result: 在最少表面基准上，单推理步将度量误差降低两个数量级；在ShapeNet上，以5倍更少步数达到SOTA，以26倍更少参数和5倍更少步数达到与DiT-3D相当的空客EMD；在单形状编码上，完全在3D空间中生成的法线和重建结果与6D生成器竞争。

Conclusion: OGPP通过粒子原生设计和对几何结构的显式利用，在粒子系统生成建模中实现了高效且高质量的流匹配，减少了所需推理步数和参数规模。

Abstract: We present Orbit-Space Geometric Probability Paths (OGPP), a particle-native flow-matching framework for generative modeling of particle systems. OGPP is motivated by two insights: (i) particles are defined up to permutation symmetries, so anonymous indexing inflates per-index target variance and yields curved, hard-to-learn flows; and (ii) particles live in physical space, so the flow terminal velocity has physical meaning and can encode geometric attributes, e.g., surface normals. OGPP instantiates three key components: (1) orbit-space canonicalization of the probability-path terminal endpoint, (2) particle index embeddings for role specialization, and (3) geometric probability paths with arc-length-aware terminal velocities that generate normals as a byproduct of the flow. We evaluate OGPP on minimal-surface benchmarks, where it reduces metric error by up to two orders of magnitude in a single inference step; on ShapeNet, where it matches the state of the art with 5x fewer steps and reaches airplane EMD comparable to DiT-3D with 26x fewer parameters and 5x fewer steps; and on single-shape encoding, where it produces normals and reconstructions competitive with 6D generators while operating entirely in 3D.

</details>


### [250] [Structural MAT: Clean and Scalable Medial Axis Simplification via Explicit Surface Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02302)
*Pengfei Wang,Shuangmin Chen,Dongming Yan,Ying He,Shiqing Xin,Changhe Tu,Wenping Wang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过显式追踪MAT顶点与表面区域对应关系，并利用优先控制策略指导边折叠顺序的网格简化方法，从而生成高质量、结构对齐的中轴变换（MAT），在运行时效率、结构对齐、边界规则性、三角形质量和噪声鲁棒性方面均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算高质量的中轴变换（MAT）仍然是一个重大挑战，特别是当输入为离散三角形网格时。理想的MAT应支持高保真重建并捕获结构特征（如与滚动球中心轨迹对齐），但现有方法难以同时满足这些要求。

Method: 遵循初始MAT通过3D Voronoi图生成的经典技术路线，随后通过类似QEM的简化方案。核心创新在于显式追踪MAT顶点与表面区域的对应关系，确保简化后的MAT三角形反映表面面片间的内在对称性，并将这些几何要求转化为控制边折叠顺序的优先策略集。

Result: 在运行时效率、结构对齐、边界规则性、三角形质量和噪声鲁棒性方面均优于现有MAT算法。生成的MAT在关节形状和CAD模型上均保持高表达能力，即使极端简化至几百个顶点也能有效捕捉复杂几何的全局结构。

Conclusion: 通过追踪对应关系和优先控制策略，成功实现了高质量、结构对齐的MAT计算，在各类模型上均表现出强鲁棒性和表达能力。

Abstract: The Medial Axis Transform (MAT) is a complete shape descriptor capable of reconstructing the geometry of the original domain. A high-quality MAT should not only facilitate high-fidelity reconstruction but also capture structural features -- for instance, by aligning the MAT boundary with the locus of rolling ball centers within fillet regions. However, computing such an ideal MAT remains a significant challenge, particularly when the input is a discrete triangle mesh.
  In this paper, we follow the established technical pipeline of initializing the MAT via a 3D Voronoi diagram of surface samples and subsequently simplifying the Voronoi structure through a QEM-like scheme. Our key insight is to explicitly track the correspondence between MAT vertices and surface regions throughout the progressive simplification process, ensuring that the resulting MAT triangles accurately reflect the intrinsic symmetries between surface patches. We translate these geometric requirements into a suite of priority control strategies that govern the sequencing of edge collapses.
  Through extensive evaluation against state-of-the-art MAT algorithms, we validate the strong performance of our approach regarding runtime efficiency, structural alignment, boundary regularity, triangle quality, and robustness to noise. Our resulting MATs remain highly expressive for both articulated shapes and CAD models, even under extreme simplification -- effectively capturing the global structure of complex geometries with only a few hundred vertices.

</details>


### [251] [Adaptive Interpolation-Synthesis for Motion In-Betweening on Keyframe-Based Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02742)
*Anton Raël,Julien Boucher,Antoine Lhermitte*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种与专业动画制作流程对齐的运动补间方法，核心是自适应插值-合成（AIS）层，通过动态平衡学习插值和直接姿态合成，并结合基于域的关键帧调度策略，在Autodesk Maya中实现了3.5倍的速度提升，达到业界领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动补间是3D动画中最耗时的创作瓶颈之一，现有深度学习方法与专业动画工作流在数据特性、运动风格和问题设定上存在脱节，亟需一种符合实际生产约束的智能辅助工具。

Method: 提出自适应插值-合成（AIS）层，动态平衡插值与姿态合成；设计基于域的关键帧调度策略，使训练分布与生产数据对齐。

Result: 在制作数据上达到SOTA性能；集成到Autodesk Maya后，动画师完成补间任务的速度提升3.5倍。

Conclusion: 与专业动画流水线对齐的方法能有效解决运动补间的生产效率瓶颈，AIS层和域调度策略是实现这一目标的关键设计。

Abstract: Motion in-betweening is one of the most artistically demanding and time consuming stages of 3D animation, where the expressivity and rhythm of motion are defined. The level of creative control it requires makes it a major production bottleneck, underscoring the need for intelligent tools that assist animators in this process. Although recent deep learning approaches have achieved strong results in motion synthesis and in-betweening, they assume data characteristics, motion styles, and problem formulations that diverge from professional animation workflows. To bridge this gap, we propose a method explicitly aligned with the constraints of motion in-betweening for keyframe-based animation in production environments. At its core, the Adaptive Interpolation-Synthesis (AIS) layer mirrors the animator's creative process by dynamically balancing learned interpolation and direct pose synthesis. In addition, a domain-based input keypose schedule reflects the distribution of production data, improving stylistic consistency and alignment between training and real-world usage. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on production data; when integrated into Autodesk Maya, it enables animators to complete in-betweening tasks with a 3.5x speedup.

</details>


### [252] [Implicit Minimal Surfaces for Bijective Correspondences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02770)
*Etienne Corman,Yousuf Soliman,Robin Magnet,Mark Gillespie*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Ginzburg-Landau泛函的隐式表示方法，用于计算亏格为零曲面间的连续、双射、保定向映射，通过优化映射曲面面积实现低畸变对应，无需显式双射初始化或障碍函数，支持点/曲线约束，相比显式方法更稳定且易于实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有曲面间双射对应计算方法通常需要障碍函数强制双射性或组合网格修改，对噪声敏感且实现复杂；同时初始化必须为双射，限制了计算效率。本文旨在提供一种更鲁棒、简单且无需显式双射初始化的隐式映射表示方法。

Method: 将两个曲面间双射视为四维乘积空间中的二维映射曲面，通过复截面（复值函数）的零集隐式编码该映射曲面；将映射畸变最小化转化为映射曲面面积最小化，等价于优化Ginzburg-Landau泛函，利用切向量场工具箱中的标准工具求解。

Result: 与最先进对应算法比较，本文方法在稳定性上表现更优；能够自动纠正由功能映射等低成本方法生成的非双射对应；自然地支持显式表示难以强加的约束（如地标点和地标曲线）。

Conclusion: 隐式表示通过Ginzburg-Landau泛函优化映射曲面面积，提供了一种鲁棒、简单且灵活的曲面间双射对应计算方案，避免了显式方法的诸多限制。

Abstract: We introduce an implicit representation of continuous, bijective, orientation-preserving maps between genus zero surfaces with or without boundary. The distortion of these maps can easily be minimized by optimizing the Ginzburg-Landau functional - a ubiquitous model in physics and differential geometry - leading to a simple algorithm for computing bijective correspondences using only standard tools of the tangent vector field toolbox. The method avoids combinatorial mesh modifications and does not require barrier functions to enforce bijectivity making it more robust to noise and simpler to implement. Moreover, the algorithm does not assume a bijective initialization and can untangle non-bijective correspondences generated by computationally cheaper methods such as functional maps. It supports the use of both landmark points and landmark curves to guide the correspondence. The key idea is that a bijection between surfaces defines a two-dimensional mapping surface sitting inside the four-dimensional product space of the two inputs, and this mapping surface can be stored implicitly as the zero set of a complex section - essentially a complex function defined on the product space. Now the distortion of the map can be optimized by minimizing the area of this mapping surface, which amounts to minimizing the Ginzburg-Landau functional of the complex section. We demonstrate the practical benefits of our method by comparing to state-of-the-art correspondence algorithms and show that our implicit representation offers improved stability and naturally supports constraints that are difficult to enforce with explicit map representations.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [253] [2026 Roadmap on Artificial Intelligence and Machine Learning for Smart Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00839)
*Jay Lee,Hanqi Su,Marco Macchi,Adalberto Polenghi,Wei Wu,Zhiheng Zhao,George Q. Huang,Kiva Allgood,Devendra Jain,Benedikt Gieger,Vibhor Pandhare,Soumyabrata Bhattacharjee,Ram Mohril,Lingbao Kong,Qiyuan Wang,Xinlan Tang,Sungjong Kim,Chan Hee Park,Byeng D. Youn,Guo Dong Goh,Xi Huang,Wai Yee Yeong,Yung C Shin,He Zhang,Zitong Wang,Fei Tao,Jagjit Singh Srai,Satyandra K. Gupta,Byung Gun Joung,Albin John,John W. Sutherland,Sang Won Lee,Olga Fink,Vinay Sharma,Faez Ahmed,Wei Chen,Mark Fuge,Arild Waaler,Martin G. Skjæveland,Dimitris Kyritsis,Wei Chen,VispiNevile Karkaria,Yi-Ping Chen,Ying-Kuan Tsai,Joseph Cohen,Xun Huan,Jing Lin,Liangwei Zhang,Gregory W. Vogl,Aaron W. Cornelius,Xiaodong Jia,Dai-Yan Ji,Takanobu Minami,Ruoxin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一份关于人工智能和机器学习在智能制造中应用的综合路线图，涵盖基础、应用和新兴方向，并指出了关键挑战与未来发展需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI/ML为智能制造带来了效率、自适应和自主性的新能力，但在工业部署中仍面临工业大数据复杂性、数据管理、异构系统集成以及高可靠性、可解释性等关键挑战，亟需系统性路线图来指导研究与实践。

Method: 路线图分为三部分：第一部分阐述AI在智能制造中的基础与趋势；第二部分聚焦AI已取得进展的关键主题（工业大数据分析、先进感知、自主系统、增材制造、数字孪生、机器人、供应链优化、可持续制造）；第三部分探索非传统ML方法的新前沿（物理信息AI、生成式AI、语义AI、高级数字孪生、可解释AI、RAMS、数据计量学、LLM及基础模型）。

Result: 识别了各领域中的机遇和现有障碍，概述了在方法、集成策略和工业采纳方面所需的进展。

Conclusion: 该路线图旨在为研究人员、工程师和从业者提供指南，加速创新，协调学术与工业优先事项，确保AI驱动的智能制造在未来制造生态系统中实现可靠、可持续和可扩展的影响。

Abstract: The evolution of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) is reshaping smart manufacturing by providing new capabilities for efficiency, adaptability, and autonomy across industrial value chains. However, the deployment of AI and ML in industrial settings still faces critical challenges, including the complexity of industrial big data, effective data management, integration with heterogeneous sensing and control systems, and the demand for trustworthy, explainable, and reliable operation in high-stakes industrial environments. In this roadmap, we present a comprehensive perspective on the foundations, applications, and emerging directions of AI and ML in smart manufacturing. It is structured in three parts. The first highlights the foundations and trends that frame the evolution of AI in smart manufacturing. The second focuses on key topics where AI is already enabling advances, including industrial big data analytics, advanced sensing and perception, autonomous systems, additive and laser-based manufacturing, digital twins, robotics, supply chain and logistics optimization, and sustainable manufacturing. The third section explores non-traditional ML approaches that are opening new frontiers, such as physics-informed AI, generative AI, semantic AI, advanced digital twins, explainable AI, RAMS, data-centric metrology, LLMs, and foundation models for highly connected and complex manufacturing systems. By identifying both opportunities and remaining barriers across these areas, this roadmap outlines the advances needed in methods, integration strategies, and industrial adoption. We hope this roadmap will serve as a guide for researchers, engineers, and practitioners to accelerate innovation, align academic and industrial priorities, and ensure that AI-driven smart manufacturing delivers reliable, sustainable, and scalable impact for the future of manufacturing ecosystems.

</details>


### [254] [Understanding Emergent Misalignment via Feature Superposition Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00842)
*Gouki Minegishi,Hiroki Furuta,Takeshi Kojima,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出基于特征叠加几何结构的解释：微调放大目标特征时，会因特征重叠表征无意中增强邻近的有害特征。通过稀疏自编码器验证了诱发失调数据特征与有害行为特征在几何上更接近，并基于几何感知的样本过滤减少了34.5%的失调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管经验证据日益增多，但微调诱导LLM产生有害行为的"新兴失调"现象的底层机制仍不清楚，需要揭示其根本原因以便理解和缓解。

Method: 1) 提出基于特征叠加几何的理论框架，给出梯度级推导；2) 在多个LLM上使用稀疏自编码器识别特征，测量诱发失调数据特征与有害特征之间的几何距离；3) 提出几何感知的样本过滤方法，移除最接近有毒特征分布的训练样本。

Result: 实验表明诱发失调数据特征与有害行为特征在几何上显著更近，该趋势跨领域（健康、职业、法律）泛化；几何感知过滤将失调降低34.5%，显著优于随机移除，效果接近或略优于基于LLM裁判的过滤。

Conclusion: 将新兴失调现象与特征叠加几何结构联系起来，为理解和缓解该现象提供了理论基础和实践方法。

Abstract: Emergent misalignment, where fine-tuning on narrow, non-harmful tasks induces harmful behaviors, poses a key challenge for AI safety in LLMs. Despite growing empirical evidence, its underlying mechanism remains unclear. To uncover the reason behind this phenomenon, we propose a geometric account based on the geometry of feature superposition. Because features are encoded in overlapping representations, fine-tuning that amplifies a target feature also unintentionally strengthens nearby harmful features in accordance with their similarity. We give a simple gradient-level derivation of this effect and empirically test it in multiple LLMs (Gemma-2 2B/9B/27B, LLaMA-3.1 8B, GPT-OSS 20B). Using sparse autoencoders (SAEs), we identify features tied to misalignment-inducing data and to harmful behaviors, and show that they are geometrically closer to each other than features derived from non-inducing data. This trend generalizes across domains (e.g., health, career, legal advice). Finally, we show that a geometry-aware approach, filtering training samples closest to toxic features, reduces misalignment by 34.5%, substantially outperforming random removal and achieving comparable or slightly lower misalignment than LLM-as-a-judge-based filtering. Our study links emergent misalignment to feature superposition, providing a basis for understanding and mitigating this phenomenon.

</details>


### [255] [ClinicBot: A Guideline-Grounded Clinical Chatbot with Prioritized Evidence RAG and Verifiable Citations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00846)
*Navapat Nananukul,Mayank Kejriwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ClinicBot是一个基于AI的临床支持系统，通过结构化提取临床指南、按临床重要性排序证据、以及提供可验证的可操作答案，解决了大语言模型在医疗场景中的幻觉问题，并在糖尿病领域基于ADA 2025标准进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床诊断需要准确、可验证且严格基于官方指南的答案，而大语言模型的幻觉问题在医疗场景中尤为严重，现有RAG系统对所有证据平等对待，产生嘈杂的上下文和与临床实践不符的通用答案。

Method: (1) 结构化提取临床指南为语义单元（建议、表格、定义、叙述）并附带明确来源；(2) 基于临床重要性和指南结构而非文本相似度的证据优先级排序；(3) 提供简洁、可操作答案及可验证证据的Web界面。在多智能体环境中运行。

Result: 使用真实患者糖尿病问题和基于ADA 2025标准的糖尿病风险评估工具进行演示，展示了语义知识提取和层次化证据排序在多智能体设置中可靠处理大规模复杂临床指南的能力。

Conclusion: ClinicBot通过结构化知识提取和层次化证据排序，能够在大规模临床指南处理中提供可验证的、可信的临床支持，为高风险的医疗决策场景提供可靠的AI解决方案。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis requires answers that are accurate, verifiable, and explicitly grounded in official guidelines. While large language models excel at natural language processing, their tendency to hallucinate undermines their utility in high-stakes medical contexts where precision is essential. Existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems treat all evidence equally, producing noisy context and generic answers misaligned with clinical practice.
